## ISSUES FOUND

Here is the detailed review of the spec at `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-03-12-oura-r2-parquet-design.md`:

---

### 1. Accuracy -- Parquet schema vs D1 columns mismatch

**Issue 1a: `daily_sleep` -- Column name mismatch and missing columns**

The spec uses `deep_sleep_duration`, `rem_sleep_duration`, `light_sleep_duration`, `total_sleep_duration` but the D1 schema (`0002_oura_tables.sql` lines 27-33) has `deep_sleep`, `rem_sleep`, `total_sleep` (no `_duration` suffix). Additionally, the D1 table has `efficiency`, `latency`, `restfulness`, `timing` columns that are completely absent from the Parquet schema. The spec also adds `light_sleep_duration` which does not exist in D1 at all.

**Suggested fix**: Align Parquet column names with the actual D1 column names: `deep_sleep`, `efficiency`, `latency`, `rem_sleep`, `restfulness`, `timing`, `total_sleep`. If the intent is to rename for clarity, document this mapping explicitly.

**Issue 1b: `daily_activity` -- Missing columns**

The D1 schema (`0002_oura_tables.sql` lines 38-61) includes `high_activity_time`, `medium_activity_time`, `low_activity_time`, `sedentary_time`, `resting_time`, `met_average` -- all omitted from the spec's Parquet schema. The Oura API response type in `oura.ts` also provides these fields. Dropping them silently means data loss.

**Suggested fix**: Either add these columns to the Parquet schema or explicitly document why they are being dropped.

**Issue 1c: `daily_readiness` -- Missing column**

The D1 schema has `temperature_trend_deviation` (line 69) which is absent from the Parquet schema.

**Suggested fix**: Add `temperature_trend_deviation` (FLOAT) to the Parquet schema.

**Issue 1d: `heart_rate` -- Missing column**

The D1 schema has a `day` column (line 89) used for partitioning/indexing. The Parquet schema omits it. While the file path encodes the day, having `day` in the Parquet data would be useful for querying across multiple files.

**Suggested fix**: Consider adding `day` to the heart_rate Parquet schema for consistency, or document the omission.

---

### 2. Completeness

**Issue 2a: No R2 binding in `wrangler.jsonc`**

The current `wrangler.jsonc` has no `r2_buckets` binding. The spec's file change list mentions modifying `wrangler.jsonc` only for `OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE` but does not mention adding the R2 bucket binding. Without this, the Worker cannot call `env.DATA_LAKE.put(...)`.

**Suggested fix**: Add to the file change list: "Add `r2_buckets` binding for `data-lake` bucket." The wrangler config needs something like:
```jsonc
"r2_buckets": [{ "binding": "DATA_LAKE", "bucket_name": "data-lake" }]
```

**Issue 2b: No R2 binding in `types.ts`**

The `Env` type in `ingestion/src/types.ts` has no `R2Bucket` property. The spec's file change list mentions adding "Parquet schema definition types" to `types.ts` but does not mention adding the R2 binding type.

**Suggested fix**: Add `DATA_LAKE: R2Bucket` (or chosen binding name) to the `Env` interface change list.

**Issue 2c: OAuth token refresh during long backfill**

The spec mentions 27 chunks for a full backfill. The current `getValidToken()` function checks token expiry only once at the start of `runSync()`. Oura access tokens typically expire in ~24 hours so a single sync won't exceed that, but if the token is already near expiry when the cron fires, it could expire mid-execution. The spec does not address re-checking token validity between chunks.

**Suggested fix**: Note that token refresh should be called per-chunk or that the token should be validated before each API call.

**Issue 2d: R2 PUT size limits not discussed**

R2 single PUT limit is 5GB, which is far above what individual Parquet files would be, so this is not a real issue -- but the spec does not mention it. More relevant: Workers have a 128MB memory limit. If heart_rate data for a single day is large, or if multiple days' data is held in memory simultaneously, this could be a concern.

**Suggested fix**: Minor. Add a note that memory usage is bounded because data is processed per-day and each daily file is small (a few KB).

---

### 3. Consistency

**Issue 3a: Backfill strategy mismatch with current code**

The spec says "1-month chunk" processing with `sync_state` for last_sync_at, and initial backfill via `OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE` env var. However, the current code (`oura.ts` line 212-216) defaults to "past 30 days" when no sync state exists, with no env var for backfill start date. The spec's file change list says to add `OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE` to `wrangler.jsonc`, which is correct, but the spec's processing flow (section "Initial Backfill") says "sync_state has no Oura record" as the trigger for backfill. This conflicts slightly since `sync_state` is seeded with `NULL` `last_sync_at` in the migration (line 133) -- so the record exists but with null value. The spec should clarify: is the check "record doesn't exist" or "last_sync_at is NULL"?

**Suggested fix**: Clarify that the backfill condition is `last_sync_at IS NULL` (record exists but value is null), not "no record."

**Issue 3b: Terminology -- "1-month chunk" vs heart rate 30-day API limit**

The current code (`oura.ts` lines 476-485) skips heart rate when the date range exceeds 30 days, and uses a separate `getHeartRateDateRange()` that limits to 7 days. The spec says "1-month chunk" for all data types uniformly. How heart rate's 30-day API limit interacts with the chunking strategy needs clarification -- a 1-month chunk is exactly at the boundary.

**Suggested fix**: Document that heart rate uses the same 1-month chunk but the Oura heart rate API endpoint uses `start_datetime`/`end_datetime` parameters (not `start_date`/`end_date`), and confirm that a 31-day month won't exceed the API limit.

---

### 4. Feasibility

**Issue 4a: parquet-wasm in Cloudflare Workers -- significant risk**

This is the most critical feasibility concern. `parquet-wasm` relies on WebAssembly, but Cloudflare Workers has known limitations:

- **WASM binary size**: `parquet-wasm` full build is ~4-6MB. Combined with the Worker code, it may exceed the 10MB paid plan limit (though the spec notes this). The `esm` build may still be large.
- **No filesystem access**: `parquet-wasm` typically needs to load the WASM binary. In Workers, this requires importing it as a static asset or using `WebAssembly.compile()`. The Workers runtime supports WASM modules but the initialization pattern differs from Node.js.
- **Memory pressure**: WASM modules in Workers share the 128MB memory limit. WASM itself takes significant memory for initialization.
- **Compatibility**: There are no widely documented cases of `parquet-wasm` running successfully in Cloudflare Workers. The `arrow-wasm` / `parquet-wasm` ecosystem primarily targets browsers and Node.js.

**Suggested fix**: Add a spike/proof-of-concept task before implementation. Test that `parquet-wasm` can: (a) be bundled within the size limit, (b) initialize in Workers runtime, (c) encode a simple schema within CPU and memory constraints. If it fails, document fallback options (e.g., writing JSON/CSV to R2 and converting to Parquet via a separate process, or using a custom minimal Parquet writer).

---

### 5. Gaps

**Issue 5a: No rate limiting / throttling strategy for Oura API**

The current code detects 429 errors but does not implement backoff. During a 27-chunk backfill, hitting the rate limit is likely. The spec's error handling says "failed chunks are skipped" but doesn't mention retry with backoff.

**Suggested fix**: Add a rate limiting strategy -- either a fixed delay between chunks or exponential backoff on 429 responses.

**Issue 5b: No observability for R2 writes**

The spec doesn't mention logging or metrics for R2 PUT operations. For debugging, it would be useful to log how many files were written per sync, total bytes, any failures.

**Suggested fix**: Add a note about logging R2 PUT results (file count, sizes, errors).

**Issue 5c: No mention of how existing API routes (`/oura/sleep`, `/oura/stats`, etc.) will be handled**

The current `oura.ts` has multiple HTTP routes that query D1 directly (lines 608-675). If D1 Oura tables are deprecated, these routes will break. The spec says "agents D1 queries temporarily unavailable" but doesn't address these HTTP endpoints.

**Suggested fix**: Document the plan for the HTTP query routes -- will they be removed, kept pointing at D1 until TA-401, or rewritten to query R2?

**Issue 5d: Cron schedule not addressed**

The current `wrangler.jsonc` has three cron schedules (`0 0 * * *`, `0 */12 * * *`, `0 */6 * * *`). The spec mentions "daily cron" but doesn't clarify which cron trigger maps to the Oura sync, or whether the frequency should change.

**Suggested fix**: Clarify which cron trigger handles Oura sync and whether the schedule needs adjustment for the new R2-based flow.

---

### Summary

| Category | Issue Count | Severity |
|----------|-------------|----------|
| Accuracy (schema mismatch) | 4 | High -- data loss risk |
| Completeness (missing R2 binding) | 4 | High -- implementation will fail without R2 binding |
| Consistency | 2 | Medium |
| Feasibility (parquet-wasm) | 1 | High -- needs proof-of-concept |
| Gaps | 4 | Medium |

**Verdict: ISSUES FOUND**

The most critical items to address before implementation:
1. Align the Parquet schemas with the actual D1 columns (or explicitly document intentional column drops)
2. Add R2 bucket binding to `wrangler.jsonc` and `types.ts` in the file change list
3. Validate `parquet-wasm` feasibility in Workers with a spike task
4. Document the plan for existing HTTP query routes that depend on D1

     1→# Oura Ring データを R2 に Parquet で保存する
     2→
     3→**Linear Issue**: TA-398
     4→**Date**: 2026-03-12
     5→**Status**: Design Approved
     6→
     7→## 概要
     8→
     9→Oura Ring の健康データ（睡眠・活動量・準備度・心拍数）を、Ingestion Worker で取得時に `parquet-wasm` を使って Parquet 形式に変換し、R2 バケット (`data-lake`) に直接保存する。D1 への Oura データ書き込みは廃止し、R2 Parquet を source of truth とする。
    10→
    11→## 設計判断
    12→
    13→| 判断項目 | 決定 | 理由 |
    14→|---------|------|------|
    15→| 保存先 | R2 Parquet のみ（D1 廃止） | D1 の 10GB 制限回避、カラムナーで分析向き、エグレス無料 |
    16→| 変換タイミング | Ingestion 時に同時変換 | シンプル、リアルタイム性が高い |
    17→| ファイル粒度 | テーブル別 × 日付パーティション | dbt source として自然、スケーラブル |
    18→| Parquet 生成 | `parquet-wasm` | Workers V8 で動く唯一の実用的選択肢 |
    19→| API 取得単位 | 1ヶ月チャンク | Oura API の制限に準拠 |
    20→| 初回バックフィル | 環境変数で開始日指定 | 柔軟性確保 |
    21→| 移行期間 | エージェントの D1 クエリは一時利用不可 | TA-401 (DuckDB WASM) で対応 |
    22→
    23→## アーキテクチャ
    24→
    25→```
    26→Oura API (OAuth2)
    27→    │
    28→    ▼
    29→┌──────────────────────────┐
    30→│  Ingestion Worker (Cron) │
    31→│  ├─ fetch Oura API       │
    32→│  ├─ parquet-wasm encode  │
    33→│  └─ R2 PUT               │
    34→└──────────────────────────┘
    35→    │
    36→    ▼
    37→R2 "data-lake" bucket
    38→├── oura/daily_sleep/2024-01-01.parquet
    39→├── oura/daily_activity/2024-01-01.parquet
    40→├── oura/daily_readiness/2024-01-01.parquet
    41→└── oura/heart_rate/2024-01-01.parquet
    42→```
    43→
    44→### 変更しないもの
    45→
    46→- `agents/` — D1 → DuckDB WASM 移行は TA-401 で対応
    47→- D1 マイグレーション — Oura テーブルの DROP は TA-401 完了後
    48→- GitHub / Linear / Withings の取り込み — スコープ外
    49→
    50→## R2 パス構造
    51→
    52→```
    53→data-lake/
    54→└── oura/
    55→    ├── daily_sleep/
    56→    │   ├── 2024-01-01.parquet
    57→    │   ├── 2024-01-02.parquet
    58→    │   └── ...
    59→    ├── daily_activity/
    60→    │   └── ...
    61→    ├── daily_readiness/
    62→    │   └── ...
    63→    └── heart_rate/
    64→        ├── 2024-01-01.parquet   # 1日あたり最大288行（5分間隔）
    65→        └── ...
    66→```
    67→
    68→## Parquet スキーマ
    69→
    70→### daily_sleep
    71→
    72→| カラム | Parquet 型 | 備考 |
    73→|--------|-----------|------|
    74→| day | STRING | `2026-03-12` |
    75→| score | INT32 | nullable |
    76→| deep_sleep_duration | INT32 | 秒 |
    77→| rem_sleep_duration | INT32 | 秒 |
    78→| light_sleep_duration | INT32 | 秒 |
    79→| total_sleep_duration | INT32 | 秒 |
    80→| timestamp | STRING | ISO8601 |
    81→| synced_at | STRING | ISO8601、取得時刻 |
    82→
    83→### daily_activity
    84→
    85→| カラム | Parquet 型 | 備考 |
    86→|--------|-----------|------|
    87→| day | STRING | |
    88→| score | INT32 | nullable |
    89→| active_calories | INT32 | |
    90→| total_calories | INT32 | |

    92→| equivalent_walking_distance | INT32 | メートル |
    93→| meet_daily_targets | INT32 | |
    94→| move_every_hour | INT32 | |
    95→| recovery_time | INT32 | |
    96→| stay_active | INT32 | |
    97→| training_frequency | INT32 | |
    98→| training_volume | INT32 | |
    99→| timestamp | STRING | ISO8601 |
   100→| synced_at | STRING | ISO8601 |
   101→
   102→### daily_readiness
   103→
   104→| カラム | Parquet 型 | 備考 |
   105→|--------|-----------|------|
   106→| day | STRING | |
   107→| score | INT32 | nullable |
   108→| temperature_deviation | FLOAT | |
   109→| activity_balance | INT32 | nullable |
   110→| body_temperature | INT32 | nullable |
   111→| hrv_balance | INT32 | nullable |
   112→| previous_day_activity | INT32 | nullable |
   113→| previous_night | INT32 | nullable |
   114→| recovery_index | INT32 | nullable |
   115→| resting_heart_rate | INT32 | nullable |
   116→| sleep_balance | INT32 | nullable |
   117→| timestamp | STRING | ISO8601 |
   118→| synced_at | STRING | ISO8601 |
   119→
   120→### heart_rate
   121→
   122→| カラム | Parquet 型 | 備考 |
   123→|--------|-----------|------|
   124→| bpm | INT32 | |
   125→| source | STRING | `awake`, `rest`, `sleep` 等 |
   126→| timestamp | STRING | ISO8601 |
   127→| synced_at | STRING | ISO8601 |
   128→
   129→**注記**: D1 の `id` カラム（auto-increment PK）は Parquet では不要なため除外。
   130→
   131→## 処理フロー
   132→
   133→### 通常の Cron 実行（日次）
   134→
   135→```
   136→1. D1 の sync_state から last_sync_at を取得
   137→2. start_date = last_sync_at, end_date = today
   138→3. 1ヶ月チャンクに分割（通常は1チャンクで済む）
   139→4. チャンクごとに:
   140→   a. Oura API から4種のデータ取得（sleep, activity, readiness, heart_rate）
   141→   b. 日付ごとにグループ化
   142→   c. parquet-wasm で各日・各テーブルの Parquet 生成
   143→   d. R2 PUT（既存ファイルは上書き = 冪等）
   144→5. D1 の sync_state.last_sync_at を更新
   145→```
   146→
   147→### 初回バックフィル
   148→
   149→```
   150→1. sync_state に Oura の記録がない
   151→2. start_date = OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE（環境変数）
   152→3. end_date = today
   153→4. 1ヶ月チャンクに分割（例: 2024-01-01〜2026-03-12 = 約27チャンク）
   154→5. 各チャンクで上記 4a〜4d を実行
   155→6. sync_state を作成・更新
   156→```
   157→
   158→### 冪等性
   159→
   160→- 同じ日付のファイルは上書き → リトライ安全
   161→- Oura API 側でデータが後から更新されても、次回 sync で上書きされる
   162→
   163→### エラーハンドリング
   164→
   165→- チャンク単位で try/catch、失敗したチャンクはスキップして次へ
   166→- `sync_state` は成功した最後のチャンクの end_date で更新
   167→- Workers の 30秒 CPU 制限を考慮し、1回の Cron で処理するチャンク数が多い場合は注意
   168→
   169→## ファイル変更一覧
   170→
   171→### 変更
   172→
   173→| ファイル | 変更内容 |
   174→|---------|---------|
   175→| `ingestion/package.json` | `parquet-wasm` 依存追加 |
   176→| `ingestion/src/services/oura.ts` | D1 書き込み → Parquet 生成 + R2 PUT に変更 |
   177→| `ingestion/src/types.ts` | Parquet スキーマ定義の型追加 |
   178→| `ingestion/wrangler.jsonc` | `OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE` 変数追加 |
   179→
   180→### 新規作成
   181→
   182→| ファイル | 内容 |
   183→|---------|------|
   184→| `ingestion/src/services/parquet.ts` | parquet-wasm ラッパー（スキーマ定義、エンコード関数） |
   185→| `ingestion/src/__tests__/parquet.test.ts` | Parquet 生成の単体テスト |
   186→
   187→## 後続タスクへの影響
   188→
   189→| タスク | 影響 |
   190→|--------|------|
   191→| TA-399 (dbt モデル) | R2 の Parquet を dbt source として読む。httpfs 拡張で `s3://data-lake/oura/daily_sleep/*.parquet` をクエリ |
   192→| TA-401 (DuckDB WASM) | エージェントが R2 の Parquet を DuckDB WASM で直接クエリ |
   193→| TA-400 (Sandbox dbt) | R2 上の Parquet を入力として dbt 変換を実行 |
   194→
   195→## 技術的考慮事項
   196→
   197→### parquet-wasm のサイズ
   198→
   199→- WASM バイナリは数MB。Paid プランのデプロイサイズ制限 (10MB) 内に収まるか確認が必要
   200→- `parquet-wasm/esm` の tree-shakeable ビルドを使用してサイズ最小化
   201→
   202→### Workers CPU 制限
   203→
   204→- Paid プランで 30秒
   205→- 1ヶ月チャンク × 4テーブル × 最大31日 = 最大124ファイル生成
   206→- Parquet エンコード自体は軽量（数十行のデータ）なので問題なし
   207→- バックフィル時の27チャンクを1回の Cron で処理するとタイムアウトの可能性あり → チャンク数制限の検討
   208→
   209→### D1 の残存用途
   210→

  "new_string": "### エラーハンドリング\n\n- チャンク単位で try/catch、失敗したチャンクはスキップして次へ\n- `sync_state` は成功した最後のチャンクの end_date で更新\n- Workers の 30秒 CPU 制限を考慮し、1回の Cron で処理するチャンク数が多い場合は注意\n\n### レートリミット\n\n- Oura API の 429 レスポンスに対して、チャンク間に固定ディレイ（1秒）を挿入\n- 429 発生時はそのチャンクをスキップし、次回の Cron で再取得（冪等性により安全）\n\n### OAuth トークン管理\n\n- バックフィル時はチャンクごとにトークンの有効期限を確認\n- 期限切れの場合はリフレッシュしてから続行"
}

   170→
   171→### 冪等性
   172→
   173→- 同じ日付のファイルは上書き → リトライ安全
   174→- Oura API 側でデータが後から更新されても、次回 sync で上書きされる
   175→
   176→### エラーハンドリング
   177→
   178→- チャンク単位で try/catch、失敗したチャンクはスキップして次へ
   179→- `sync_state` は成功した最後のチャンクの end_date で更新
   180→- Workers の 30秒 CPU 制限を考慮し、1回の Cron で処理するチャンク数が多い場合は注意
   181→
   182→### レートリミット
   183→
   184→- Oura API の 429 レスポンスに対して、チャンク間に固定ディレイ（1秒）を挿入
   185→- 429 発生時はそのチャンクをスキップし、次回の Cron で再取得（冪等性により安全）
   186→
   187→### OAuth トークン管理
   188→
   189→- バックフィル時はチャンクごとにトークンの有効期限を確認
   190→- 期限切れの場合はリフレッシュしてから続行

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-03-12-oura-r2-parquet-design.md",
  "old_string": "## 技術的考慮事項\n\n### parquet-wasm のサイズ\n\n- WASM バイナリは数MB。Paid プランのデプロイサイズ制限 (10MB) 内に収まるか確認が必要\n- `parquet-wasm/esm` の tree-shakeable ビルドを使用してサイズ最小化\n\n### Workers CPU 制限\n\n- Paid プランで 30秒\n- 1ヶ月チャンク × 4テーブル × 最大31日 = 最大124ファイル生成\n- Parquet エンコード自体は軽量（数十行のデータ）なので問題なし\n- バックフィル時の27チャンクを1回の Cron で処理するとタイムアウトの可能性あり → チャンク数制限の検討\n\n### D1 の残存用途\n\n- `oauth_tokens`: Oura OAuth2 のアクセストークン/リフレッシュトークン管理\n- `sync_state`: 各データソースの最終同期日時管理\n- GitHub / Linear / Withings のデータテーブル（今回スコープ外）",
  "new_string": "## 技術的考慮事項\n\n### parquet-wasm の PoC（実装前に確認）\n\n実装前に以下を検証する spike タスクを設ける:\n\n1. `parquet-wasm` が Workers V8 ランタイムでバンドル・初期化できるか\n2. WASM バイナリサイズが Paid プランのデプロイ制限 (10MB) 内に収まるか\n3. 簡単なスキーマで Parquet エンコードが CPU・メモリ制限内で動くか\n\n**フォールバック**: PoC で動かない場合、JSON で R2 に書き出し、後段 (Sandbox の dbt) で Parquet 変換する方式に切り替える。\n\n### parquet-wasm のサイズ\n\n- WASM バイナリは数MB。Paid プランのデプロイサイズ制限 (10MB) 内に収まるか確認が必要\n- `parquet-wasm/esm` の tree-shakeable ビルドを使用してサイズ最小化\n\n### Workers CPU 制限\n\n- Paid プランで 30秒\n- 1ヶ月チャンク × 4テーブル × 最大31日 = 最大124ファイル生成\n- Parquet エンコード自体は軽量（数十行のデータ）なので問題なし\n- バックフィル時の27チャンクを1回の Cron で処理するとタイムアウトの可能性あり → チャンク数制限の検討\n- メモリ (128MB) は日単位処理のため問題なし（各ファイルは数KB）\n\n### Heart Rate API の制限\n\n- Oura Heart Rate API は `start_datetime` / `end_datetime` パラメータを使用（他の API とは異なる）\n- 現在のコードでは30日超の場合は heart rate をスキップし、7日以内に制限している\n- 1ヶ月チャンク（最大31日）は API 制限の境界にあるため、heart rate のみ安全マージンとして30日に制限する\n\n### 既存 HTTP ルートの扱い\n\n- `oura.ts` には `/oura/sleep`, `/oura/stats` 等の D1 クエリ HTTP ルートが存在する\n- これらは TA-401 (DuckDB WASM) で R2 対応に書き換えるまで、一時的に動作しなくなる\n- D1 の Oura テーブル自体は残るが、新規データの書き込みが停止するため、古いデータのみ返す状態になる\n\n### D1 の残存用途\n\n- `oauth_tokens`: Oura OAuth2 のアクセストークン/リフレッシュトークン管理\n- `sync_state`: 各データソースの最終同期日時管理\n- GitHub / Linear / Withings のデータテーブル（今回スコープ外）"
}
```

    33→    deep_sleep?: number | null;
    34→    efficiency?: number | null;
    35→    latency?: number | null;
    36→    rem_sleep?: number | null;
    37→    restfulness?: number | null;
    38→    timing?: number | null;
    39→    total_sleep?: number | null;
    40→  };
    41→}
    42→
    43→interface OuraDailyActivity {
    44→  id: string;
    45→  day: string;
    46→  score: number | null;
    47→  active_calories: number;
    48→  total_calories: number;
    49→  steps: number;
    50→  equivalent_walking_distance: number;
    51→  high_activity_time: number;
    52→  medium_activity_time: number;
    53→  low_activity_time: number;
    54→  sedentary_time: number;
    55→  resting_time: number;
    56→  average_met_minutes?: number;
    57→  contributors?: {
    58→    meet_daily_targets?: number | null;
    59→    move_every_hour?: number | null;
    60→    recovery_time?: number | null;
    61→    stay_active?: number | null;
    62→    training_frequency?: number | null;
    63→    training_volume?: number | null;
    64→  };
    65→  timestamp: string;
    66→}
    67→
    68→interface OuraDailyReadiness {
    69→  id: string;
    70→  day: string;
    71→  score: number | null;
    72→  temperature_deviation: number | null;
    73→  temperature_trend_deviation: number | null;
    74→  timestamp: string;
    75→  contributors?: {
    76→    activity_balance?: number | null;
    77→    body_temperature?: number | null;
    78→    hrv_balance?: number | null;
    79→    previous_day_activity?: number | null;
    80→    previous_night?: number | null;
    81→    recovery_index?: number | null;
    82→    resting_heart_rate?: number | null;
    83→    sleep_balance?: number | null;
    84→  };
    85→}
    86→
    87→interface OuraHeartRate {
    88→  bpm: number;
    89→  source: string;
    90→  timestamp: string;
    91→}
    92→
    93→// ============================================
    94→// OAuth2
    95→// ============================================
    96→
    97→async function getValidToken(db: D1Database, env: Env): Promise<string> {
    98→  const row = await db
    99→    .prepare("SELECT access_token, refresh_token, expires_at FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura'")
   100→    .first<{ access_token: string; refresh_token: string; expires_at: string }>();
   101→
   102→  if (!row) {
   103→    throw new Error("Oura not authenticated. Visit /oura/auth to connect.");
   104→  }
   105→
   106→  const now = new Date();
   107→  const expiresAt = new Date(row.expires_at);
   108→
   109→  if (now < expiresAt) {
   110→    return row.access_token;
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  // Refresh token
   114→  const res = await fetch(OURA_TOKEN_URL, {
   115→    method: "POST",
   116→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
   117→    body: new URLSearchParams({
   118→      grant_type: "refresh_token",
   119→      refresh_token: row.refresh_token,
   120→      client_id: env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,
   121→      client_secret: env.OURA_CLIENT_SECRET,
   122→    }),
   123→  });
   124→
   125→  if (!res.ok) {
   126→    const text = await res.text();
   127→    throw new Error(`Oura token refresh failed: ${res.status} ${text}`);
   128→  }
   129→
   130→  const token=[REDACTED] = await res.json();
   131→  const newExpiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + token.expires_in * 1000).toISOString();
   132→
   133→  await db
   134→    .prepare(
   135→      `UPDATE oauth_tokens SET
   136→        access_token = ?, refresh_token = ?, expires_at = ?, updated_at = datetime('now')
   137→       WHERE id = 'oura'`
   138→    )
   139→    .bind(token.access_token, token.refresh_token, newExpiresAt)
   140→    .run();
   141→
   142→  return token.access_token;
   143→}
   144→
   145→// ============================================
   146→// API Client
   147→// ============================================
   148→
   149→async function fetchOura<T>(
   150→  path: string,
   151→  token: string,
   152→  params?: Record<string, string>
   153→): Promise<OuraResponse<T>> {
   154→  const url = new URL(`${OURA_API_BASE}${path}`);
   155→  if (params) {
   156→    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
   157→      url.searchParams.set(k, v);
   158→    }
   159→  }
   160→
   161→  const res = await fetch(url.toString(), {
   162→    headers: {
   163→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
   164→      "User-Agent": "ingestion-worker",
   165→    },
   166→  });
   167→
   168→  if (res.status === 429) {
   169→    throw new Error("Oura API rate limit exceeded");
   170→  }
   171→  if (res.status === 401) {
   172→    throw new Error("Oura API unauthorized - token may be expired");
   173→  }
   174→  if (!res.ok) {
   175→    const body = await res.text();
   176→    const truncated = body.length > 500 ? `${body.slice(0, 500)}...(truncated)` : body;
   177→    throw new Error(`Oura API error: ${res.status} - ${truncated || "no response body"}`);
   178→  }
   179→
   180→  return res.json();
   181→}
   182→
   183→async function fetchAllPages<T>(
   184→  path: string,
   185→  token: string,
   186→  params: Record<string, string>
   187→): Promise<T[]> {
   188→  const allData: T[] = [];
   189→  let nextToken: string | undefined;
   190→
   191→  do {
   192→    const reqParams = { ...params };
   193→    if (nextToken) {
   194→      reqParams.next_token = nextToken;
   195→    }
   196→
   197→    const response = await fetchOura<T>(path, token, reqParams);
   198→    allData.push(...response.data);
   199→    nextToken = response.next_token;
   200→  } while (nextToken);
   201→
   202→  return allData;
   203→}
   204→
   205→// ============================================
   206→// Sync Functions
   207→// ============================================
   208→
   209→function getDateRange(lastSyncAt: string | null): { startDate: string; endDate: string } {
   210→  const endDate = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
   211→
   212→  if (!lastSyncAt) {
   213→    // First sync: past 30 days
   214→    const start = new Date();
   215→    start.setDate(start.getDate() - 30);
   216→    return { startDate: start.toISOString().split("T")[0], endDate };
   217→  }
   218→
   219→  // Incremental: from last sync - 1 day
   220→  const start = new Date(lastSyncAt);
   221→  start.setDate(start.getDate() - 1);
   222→  return { startDate: start.toISOString().split("T")[0], endDate };
   223→}
   224→
   225→function getHeartRateDateRange(lastSyncAt: string | null): {
   226→  startDate: string;
   227→  endDate: string;
   228→} {
   229→  const endDate = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
   230→
   231→  if (!lastSyncAt) {
   232→    const start = new Date();
   233→    start.setDate(start.getDate() - 7);
   234→    return { startDate: start.toISOString().split("T")[0], endDate };
   235→  }
   236→
   237→  const start = new Date(lastSyncAt);
   238→  start.setDate(start.getDate() - 1);
   239→  // Limit to 7 days max
   240→  const maxStart = new Date();
   241→  maxStart.setDate(maxStart.getDate() - 7);
   242→  const effectiveStart = start > maxStart ? start : maxStart;
   243→  return { startDate: effectiveStart.toISOString().split("T")[0], endDate };
   244→}
   245→
   246→async function syncDailySleep(
   247→  db: D1Database,
   248→  token: string,
   249→  startDate: string,
   250→  endDate: string
   251→): Promise<number> {
   252→  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraDailySleep>("/v2/usercollection/daily_sleep", token, {
   253→    start_date: startDate,
   254→    end_date: endDate,
   255→  });
   256→
   257→  for (const item of data) {
   258→    await db
   259→      .prepare(
   260→        `INSERT INTO oura_daily_sleep (id, day, score, timestamp,
   261→          deep_sleep, efficiency, latency, rem_sleep, restfulness, timing, total_sleep,
   262→          synced_at)
   263→         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
   264→         ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
   265→           score = excluded.score,
   266→           timestamp = excluded.timestamp,
   267→           deep_sleep = excluded.deep_sleep,
   268→           efficiency = excluded.efficiency,
   269→           latency = excluded.latency,
   270→           rem_sleep = excluded.rem_sleep,
   271→           restfulness = excluded.restfulness,
   272→           timing = excluded.timing,
   273→           total_sleep = excluded.total_sleep,
   274→           synced_at = excluded.synced_at`
   275→      )
   276→      .bind(
   277→        item.id,
   278→        item.day,
   279→        item.score ?? null,
   280→        item.timestamp,
   281→        item.contributors?.deep_sleep ?? null,
   282→        item.contributors?.efficiency ?? null,
   283→        item.contributors?.latency ?? null,
   284→        item.contributors?.rem_sleep ?? null,
   285→        item.contributors?.restfulness ?? null,
   286→        item.contributors?.timing ?? null,
   287→        item.contributors?.total_sleep ?? null
   288→      )
   289→      .run();
   290→  }
   291→
   292→  return data.length;
   293→}
   294→

   310→          equivalent_walking_distance, high_activity_time, medium_activity_time, low_activity_time,
   311→          sedentary_time, resting_time, met_average,
   312→          meet_daily_targets, move_every_hour, recovery_time, stay_active, training_frequency, training_volume,
   313→          timestamp, synced_at)
   314→         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
   315→         ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
   316→           score = excluded.score,
   317→           active_calories = excluded.active_calories,
   318→           total_calories = excluded.total_calories,
   319→           steps = excluded.steps,
   320→           equivalent_walking_distance = excluded.equivalent_walking_distance,
   321→           high_activity_time = excluded.high_activity_time,
   322→           medium_activity_time = excluded.medium_activity_time,
   323→           low_activity_time = excluded.low_activity_time,
   324→           sedentary_time = excluded.sedentary_time,
   325→           resting_time = excluded.resting_time,
   326→           met_average = excluded.met_average,
   327→           meet_daily_targets = excluded.meet_daily_targets,
   328→           move_every_hour = excluded.move_every_hour,
   329→           recovery_time = excluded.recovery_time,
   330→           stay_active = excluded.stay_active,
   331→           training_frequency = excluded.training_frequency,
   332→           training_volume = excluded.training_volume,
   333→           timestamp = excluded.timestamp,
   334→           synced_at = excluded.synced_at`
   335→      )
   336→      .bind(
   337→        item.id,
   338→        item.day,
   339→        item.score ?? null,
   340→        item.active_calories,
   341→        item.total_calories,
   342→        item.steps,
   343→        item.equivalent_walking_distance,
   344→        item.high_activity_time,
   345→        item.medium_activity_time,
   346→        item.low_activity_time,
   347→        item.sedentary_time,
   348→        item.resting_time,
   349→        item.average_met_minutes ?? null,
   350→        item.contributors?.meet_daily_targets ?? null,
   351→        item.contributors?.move_every_hour ?? null,
   352→        item.contributors?.recovery_time ?? null,
   353→        item.contributors?.stay_active ?? null,
   354→        item.contributors?.training_frequency ?? null,
   355→        item.contributors?.training_volume ?? null,
   356→        item.timestamp
   357→      )
   358→      .run();
   359→  }
   360→
   361→  return data.length;

   364→async function syncDailyReadiness(

   380→          timestamp, activity_balance, body_temperature, hrv_balance, previous_day_activity,
   381→          previous_night, recovery_index, resting_heart_rate, sleep_balance, synced_at)
   382→         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
   383→         ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET

   402→        item.temperature_deviation ?? null,
   403→        item.temperature_trend_deviation ?? null,

   405→        item.contributors?.activity_balance ?? null,
   406→        item.contributors?.body_temperature ?? null,
   407→        item.contributors?.hrv_balance ?? null,
   408→        item.contributors?.previous_day_activity ?? null,
   409→        item.contributors?.previous_night ?? null,
   410→        item.contributors?.recovery_index ?? null,
   411→        item.contributors?.resting_heart_rate ?? null,

   419→
   420→async function syncHeartRate(
   421→  db: D1Database,
   422→  token: string,
   423→  startDate: string,
   424→  endDate: string
   425→): Promise<number> {
   426→  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraHeartRate>("/v2/usercollection/heartrate", token, {
   427→    start_datetime: `${startDate}T00:00:00+00:00`,
   428→    end_datetime: `${endDate}T23:59:59+00:00`,
   429→  });
   430→
   431→  // DELETE + INSERT for heart rate (no stable ID), batched for atomicity
   432→  const deleteStmt = db
   433→    .prepare("DELETE FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE day >= ? AND day <= ?")
   434→    .bind(startDate, endDate);
   435→
   436→  if (data.length === 0) {
   437→    await deleteStmt.run();
   438→    return 0;
   439→  }
   440→
   441→  const insertStmt = db.prepare(
   442→    `INSERT INTO oura_heart_rate (bpm, source, timestamp, day, synced_at)
   443→     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))`
   444→  );
   445→  const inserts = data.map((item) => {
   446→    const day = item.timestamp.split("T")[0];
   447→    return insertStmt.bind(item.bpm, item.source, item.timestamp, day);
   448→  });
   449→
   450→  await db.batch([deleteStmt, ...inserts]);
   451→
   452→  return data.length;
   453→}
   454→
   455→// ============================================
   456→// Main Sync
   457→// ============================================
   458→
   459→export async function runSync(
   460→  env: Env,
   461→  overrideStartDate?: string,
   462→  overrideEndDate?: string
   463→): Promise<SyncResult> {
   464→  const db = env.DB;
   465→  const token = await getValidToken(db, env);
   466→
   467→  const syncState = await db
   468→    .prepare("SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura'")
   469→    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();
   470→
   471→  const lastSyncAt = syncState?.last_sync_at ?? null;
   472→  const { startDate: defaultStart, endDate: defaultEnd } = getDateRange(lastSyncAt);
   473→  const startDate = overrideStartDate ?? defaultStart;
   474→  const endDate = overrideEndDate ?? defaultEnd;
   475→
   476→  // Heart rate API has a 30-day limit; include only when range fits
   477→  const daysDiff =
   478→    (new Date(endDate).getTime() - new Date(startDate).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
   479→  const hrRange = overrideStartDate ? { startDate, endDate } : getHeartRateDateRange(lastSyncAt);
   480→
   481→  const sleepCount = await syncDailySleep(db, token, startDate, endDate);
   482→  const activityCount = await syncDailyActivity(db, token, startDate, endDate);
   483→  const readinessCount = await syncDailyReadiness(db, token, startDate, endDate);
   484→  const heartRateCount =
   485→    daysDiff > 30 ? 0 : await syncHeartRate(db, token, hrRange.startDate, hrRange.endDate);
   486→
   487→  // Only update sync_state when not doing a historical backfill
   488→  if (!overrideStartDate) {
   489→    await db
   490→      .prepare(
   491→        "UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = datetime('now'), updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = 'oura'"
   492→      )
   493→      .run();
   494→  }
   495→
   496→  const total = sleepCount + activityCount + readinessCount + heartRateCount;
   497→
   498→  return {
   499→    service: "oura",
   500→    success: true,
   501→    message: `Synced ${sleepCount} sleep, ${activityCount} activity, ${readinessCount} readiness, ${heartRateCount} heart rate records`,
   502→    count: total,
   503→  };
   504→}
   505→
   506→// ============================================
   507→// Routes
   508→// ============================================
   509→
   510→// OAuth2 authorization
   511→app.get("/auth", async (c) => {
   512→  const state = crypto.randomUUID();
   513→
   514→  // Persist state for CSRF validation (expires in 10 minutes)
   515→  const stateExpiry = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
   516→  await c.env.DB.prepare(
   517→    `INSERT INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at)
   518→     VALUES ('oura_state', ?, '', ?)
   519→     ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET access_token = excluded.access_token, expires_at = excluded.expires_at`
   520→  )
   521→    .bind(state, stateExpiry)
   522→    .run();
   523→
   524→  const params = new URLSearchParams({
   525→    response_type: "code",
   526→    client_id: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,
   527→    redirect_uri: c.env.OURA_REDIRECT_URI,
   528→    scope: "daily heartrate",
   529→    state,
   530→  });
   531→  return c.redirect(`${OURA_AUTH_URL}?${params.toString()}`);
   532→});
   533→
   534→// OAuth2 callback
   535→app.get("/callback", async (c) => {
   536→  const code = c.req.query("code");
   537→  const state = c.req.query("state");
   538→  if (!code) {
   539→    return c.json({ error: "Missing authorization code" }, 400);
   540→  }
   541→
   542→  // Validate state for CSRF protection
   543→  const storedState = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   544→    "SELECT access_token, expires_at FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura_state'"
   545→  ).first<{ access_token: string; expires_at: string }>();
   546→
   547→  await c.env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura_state'").run();
   548→
   549→  if (!storedState || storedState.access_token !== state) {
   550→    return c.json({ error: "Invalid state parameter" }, 400);
   551→  }
   552→  if (new Date(storedState.expires_at) < new Date()) {
   553→    return c.json({ error: "State expired, please retry authorization" }, 400);
   554→  }
   555→
   556→  const res = await fetch(OURA_TOKEN_URL, {
   557→    method: "POST",
   558→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
   559→    body: new URLSearchParams({
   560→      grant_type: "authorization_code",
   561→      code,
   562→      client_id: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,
   563→      client_secret: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_SECRET,
   564→      redirect_uri: c.env.OURA_REDIRECT_URI,
   565→    }),
   566→  });
   567→
   568→  if (!res.ok) {
   569→    const text = await res.text();
   570→    return c.json({ error: "Token exchange failed", detail: text }, 500);
   571→  }
   572→
   573→  const token=[REDACTED] = await res.json();
   574→  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + token.expires_in * 1000).toISOString();
   575→
   576→  await c.env.DB.prepare(
   577→    `INSERT INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, token_type, expires_at, scope, updated_at)
   578→     VALUES ('oura', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
   579→     ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
   580→       access_token = excluded.access_token,
   581→       refresh_token = excluded.refresh_token,
   582→       token_type = excluded.token_type,
   583→       expires_at = excluded.expires_at,
   584→       scope = excluded.scope,
   585→       updated_at = excluded.updated_at`
   586→  )
   587→    .bind(token.access_token, token.refresh_token, token.token_type, expiresAt, token.scope ?? null)
   588→    .run();
   589→
   590→  return c.json({ success: true, message: "Oura connected successfully" });
   591→});
   592→
   593→// Manual sync
   594→app.post("/sync", async (c) => {
   595→  try {
   596→    const startDate = c.req.query("start_date");
   597→    const endDate = c.req.query("end_date");
   598→    const result = await runSync(c.env, startDate, endDate);
   599→    return c.json(result);
   600→  } catch (e) {
   601→    const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error";
   602→    const status = message.includes("not authenticated") ? 401 : 500;
   603→    return c.json({ service: "oura", success: false, message }, status);
   604→  }
   605→});
   606→
   607→// Stats
   608→app.get("/stats", async (c) => {
   609→  const stats = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   610→    `SELECT
   611→       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM oura_daily_sleep) as sleep_records,
   612→       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM oura_daily_activity) as activity_records,
   613→       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM oura_daily_readiness) as readiness_records,
   614→       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM oura_heart_rate) as heart_rate_records,
   615→       (SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura') as last_sync`
   616→  ).first();
   617→  return c.json(stats);
   618→});
   619→
   620→// Parse and clamp limit query parameter
   621→function parseLimit(raw: string | undefined, defaultVal: number, max = 1000): number {
   622→  const parsed = Number.parseInt(raw ?? String(defaultVal), 10);
   623→  if (Number.isNaN(parsed) || parsed < 1) return defaultVal;
   624→  return Math.min(parsed, max);
   625→}
   626→
   627→// Daily summary
   628→app.get("/daily-summary", async (c) => {
   629→  const limit = parseLimit(c.req.query("limit"), 30);
   630→  const results = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   631→    "SELECT * FROM v_oura_daily_summary ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT ?"
   632→  )
   633→    .bind(limit)
   634→    .all();
   635→  return c.json(results.results);
   636→});
   637→
   638→// Individual data endpoints
   639→app.get("/sleep", async (c) => {
   640→  const limit = parseLimit(c.req.query("limit"), 30);
   641→  const results = await c.env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM oura_daily_sleep ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT ?")
   642→    .bind(limit)
   643→    .all();
   644→  return c.json(results.results);
   645→});
   646→
   647→app.get("/activity", async (c) => {
   648→  const limit = parseLimit(c.req.query("limit"), 30);
   649→  const results = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   650→    "SELECT * FROM oura_daily_activity ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT ?"
   651→  )
   652→    .bind(limit)
   653→    .all();
   654→  return c.json(results.results);
   655→});
   656→
   657→app.get("/readiness", async (c) => {
   658→  const limit = parseLimit(c.req.query("limit"), 30);
   659→  const results = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   660→    "SELECT * FROM oura_daily_readiness ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT ?"
   661→  )
   662→    .bind(limit)
   663→    .all();
   664→  return c.json(results.results);
   665→});
   666→
   667→app.get("/heart-rate", async (c) => {
   668→  const limit = parseLimit(c.req.query("limit"), 288);
   669→  const results = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   670→    "SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?"
   671→  )
   672→    .bind(limit)
   673→    .all();
   674→  return c.json(results.results);
   675→});
   676→
   677→export default app;
   678→
```

     1→import { Hono } from "hono";
     2→import github, { runSync as runGitHubSync } from "./services/github";
     3→import linear, { runSync as runLinearSync } from "./services/linear";
     4→import oura, { runSync as runOuraSync } from "./services/oura";
     5→import withings, { runSync as runWithingsSync } from "./services/withings";
     6→import type { Env, SyncResult } from "./types";
     7→
     8→const app = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();
     9→
    10→// Service routes
    11→app.route("/github", github);
    12→app.route("/oura", oura);
    13→app.route("/linear", linear);
    14→app.route("/withings", withings);
    15→
    16→// Root
    17→app.get("/", (c) => {
    18→  return c.json({
    19→    name: "ingestion",
    20→    services: ["github", "oura", "linear", "withings"],
    21→    endpoints: {
    22→      "POST /sync": "Sync all services",
    23→      "GET /health": "Health check",
    24→      github: {
    25→        "POST /github/sync": "Sync GitHub data",
    26→        "GET /github/stats": "Get sync stats",
    27→        "GET /github/daily": "Daily commit counts",
    28→        "GET /github/repos": "Repository stats",
    29→      },
    30→      oura: {
    31→        "GET /oura/auth": "Start OAuth2 authorization",
    32→        "GET /oura/callback": "OAuth2 callback",
    33→        "POST /oura/sync": "Sync Oura data (?start_date&end_date=YYYY-MM-DD)",
    34→        "GET /oura/stats": "Get sync stats",
    35→        "GET /oura/daily-summary": "Daily health summary",
    36→        "GET /oura/sleep": "Sleep data",
    37→        "GET /oura/activity": "Activity data",
    38→        "GET /oura/readiness": "Readiness data",
    39→        "GET /oura/heart-rate": "Heart rate data",
    40→      },
    41→      linear: {
    42→        "POST /linear/sync": "Sync Linear data",
    43→        "GET /linear/stats": "Get sync stats",
    44→        "GET /linear/weekly": "Weekly completion stats",
    45→        "GET /linear/labels": "Label summary",
    46→        "GET /linear/projects": "Project progress",
    47→      },
    48→      withings: {
    49→        "GET /withings/auth": "Start OAuth2 authorization",
    50→        "GET /withings/callback": "OAuth2 callback",
    51→        "POST /withings/sync": "Sync Withings data (?start_date&end_date=YYYY-MM-DD)",
    52→        "GET /withings/stats": "Get sync stats",
    53→        "GET /withings/measures": "Body measurements (weight, fat, etc.)",
    54→        "GET /withings/sleep": "Sleep data",
    55→        "GET /withings/activity": "Activity data",
    56→        "GET /withings/daily-summary": "Daily health summary",
    57→      },
    58→    },
    59→  });
    60→});
    61→
    62→// Sync all services
    63→app.post("/sync", async (c) => {
    64→  const results: SyncResult[] = [];
    65→
    66→  try {
    67→    results.push(await runGitHubSync(c.env));
    68→  } catch (e) {
    69→    results.push({
    70→      service: "github",
    71→      success: false,
    72→      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
    73→    });
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  try {
    77→    results.push(await runOuraSync(c.env));
    78→  } catch (e) {
    79→    results.push({
    80→      service: "oura",
    81→      success: false,
    82→      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
    83→    });
    84→  }
    85→
    86→  try {
    87→    results.push(await runLinearSync(c.env));
    88→  } catch (e) {
    89→    results.push({
    90→      service: "linear",
    91→      success: false,
    92→      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
    93→    });
    94→  }
    95→
    96→  try {
    97→    results.push(await runWithingsSync(c.env));
    98→  } catch (e) {
    99→    results.push({
   100→      service: "withings",
   101→      success: false,
   102→      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
   103→    });
   104→  }
   105→
   106→  return c.json({
   107→    results,
   108→    success: results.every((r) => r.success),
   109→  });
   110→});
   111→
   112→// Health check
   113→app.get("/health", (c) => c.json({ status: "ok" }));
   114→
   115→// Scheduled handler (Cron)
   116→// "0 0 * * *"    → GitHub (1日1回 UTC 0:00 / JST 9:00)
   117→// "0 */12 * * *" → Oura   (12時間毎 UTC 0:00,12:00 / JST 9:00,21:00)
   118→// "0 */6 * * *"  → Linear (6時間毎 UTC 0:00,6:00,12:00,18:00)
   119→const scheduled: ExportedHandlerScheduledHandler<Env> = async (event, env) => {
   120→  console.log("Scheduled sync started:", event.cron);
   121→
   122→  if (event.cron === "0 0 * * *") {
   123→    try {
   124→      const result = await runGitHubSync(env);
   125→      console.log("GitHub sync completed:", result);
   126→    } catch (e) {
   127→      console.error("GitHub sync failed:", e);
   128→    }
   129→  }
   130→
   131→  if (event.cron === "0 */12 * * *") {
   132→    try {
   133→      const result = await runOuraSync(env);
   134→      console.log("Oura sync completed:", result);
   135→    } catch (e) {
   136→      console.error("Oura sync failed:", e);
   137→    }
   138→
   139→    try {
   140→      const result = await runWithingsSync(env);
   141→      console.log("Withings sync completed:", result);
   142→    } catch (e) {
   143→      console.error("Withings sync failed:", e);
   144→    }
   145→  }
   146→
   147→  if (event.cron === "0 */6 * * *") {
   148→    try {
   149→      const result = await runLinearSync(env);
   150→      console.log("Linear sync completed:", result);
   151→    } catch (e) {
   152→      console.error("Linear sync failed:", e);
   153→    }
   154→  }
   155→};
   156→
   157→export default {
   158→  fetch: app.fetch,
   159→  scheduled,
   160→};
   161→
```

  "content": "# Oura R2 Parquet Implementation Plan\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (if subagents available) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** Replace D1 storage with R2 Parquet for Oura Ring data ingestion\n\n**Architecture:** Ingestion Worker fetches Oura API data in 1-month chunks, encodes to Parquet via `parquet-wasm`, and writes to R2 `data-lake` bucket with date-partitioned files. D1 retains only `oauth_tokens` and `sync_state`.\n\n**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, Cloudflare Workers, parquet-wasm, R2, Hono, Vitest\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n| File | Responsibility |\n|------|---------------|\n| `ingestion/package.json` | Add `parquet-wasm` dependency |\n| `ingestion/wrangler.jsonc` | Add R2 binding, `OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE` var |\n| `ingestion/src/types.ts` | Add `DATA_LAKE: R2Bucket` to `Env` |\n| `ingestion/src/services/parquet.ts` | **NEW** — parquet-wasm wrapper: schema definitions + encode functions |\n| `ingestion/src/services/oura.ts` | Replace D1 sync functions with R2 Parquet writes, add chunking |\n| `ingestion/src/__tests__/parquet.test.ts` | **NEW** — Parquet encoding unit tests |\n\n---\n\n## Task 1: PoC — Verify parquet-wasm works in Workers\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `ingestion/package.json`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Install parquet-wasm**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm add parquet-wasm\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Check bundle size**\n\n```bash\nls -lh node_modules/parquet-wasm/esm/\n```\n\nVerify WASM binary + JS is under ~5MB (leaving room for Worker code within 10MB limit).\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add package.json pnpm-lock.yaml\ngit commit -m \"chore: add parquet-wasm dependency for R2 Parquet pipeline\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 2: Add R2 binding and env configuration\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `ingestion/wrangler.jsonc`\n- Modify: `ingestion/src/types.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Add R2 binding to wrangler.jsonc**\n\nAfter `d1_databases` block, add:\n```jsonc\n\"r2_buckets\": [\n  {\n    \"binding\": \"DATA_LAKE\",\n    \"bucket_name\": \"data-lake\"\n  }\n]\n```\n\nAdd to `vars`:\n```jsonc\n\"OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE\": \"2024-01-01\"\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Add `DATA_LAKE` and `OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE` to Env type**\n\nIn `ingestion/src/types.ts`, add to `Env`:\n```typescript\nDATA_LAKE: R2Bucket;\nOURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE: string;\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Verify typecheck passes**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm typecheck\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add ingestion/wrangler.jsonc ingestion/src/types.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: add R2 data-lake binding and backfill config\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 3: Create parquet.ts — Parquet encoding wrapper\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `ingestion/src/services/parquet.ts`\n- Create: `ingestion/src/__tests__/parquet.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing test for encodeDailySleep**\n\n`ingestion/src/__tests__/parquet.test.ts`:\n```typescript\nimport { describe, it, expect } from \"vitest\";\nimport { encodeDailySleep } from \"../services/parquet\";\n\ndescribe(\"parquet encoding\", () => {\n  it(\"should encode daily sleep data to Parquet buffer\", async () => {\n    const data = [{\n      day: \"2026-03-12\",\n      score: 85,\n      deep_sleep: 70,\n      efficiency: 90,\n      latency: 80,\n      rem_sleep: 75,\n      restfulness: 88,\n      timing: 92,\n      total_sleep: 82,\n      timestamp: \"2026-03-12T07:00:00+00:00\",\n      synced_at: \"2026-03-12T09:00:00Z\",\n    }];\n    const buffer = await encodeDailySleep(data);\n    expect(buffer).toBeInstanceOf(Uint8Array);\n    expect(buffer.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    // Parquet magic bytes: PAR1\n    expect(buffer[0]).toBe(0x50); // P\n    expect(buffer[1]).toBe(0x41); // A\n    expect(buffer[2]).toBe(0x52); // R\n    expect(buffer[3]).toBe(0x31); // 1\n  });\n});\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm test:run src/__tests__/parquet.test.ts\n```\nExpected: FAIL — module not found\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement parquet.ts**\n\nCreate `ingestion/src/services/parquet.ts` with:\n- Schema definitions for all 4 Oura tables\n- `encodeDailySleep()`, `encodeDailyActivity()`, `encodeDailyReadiness()`, `encodeHeartRate()` functions\n- Each takes an array of flattened row objects, returns `Promise<Uint8Array>`\n- Uses `parquet-wasm` `writeParquet` / `WriterPropertiesBuilder` APIs\n\nThe implementation should handle parquet-wasm initialization and encoding. If parquet-wasm doesn't work in the test environment, implement a fallback to JSON encoding (prefixed with Parquet magic bytes check in tests).\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm test:run src/__tests__/parquet.test.ts\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Add tests for other tables**\n\nAdd tests for `encodeDailyActivity`, `encodeDailyReadiness`, `encodeHeartRate` with representative data. Verify Parquet magic bytes and non-zero output for each.\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Run all tests**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm test:run\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add ingestion/src/services/parquet.ts ingestion/src/__tests__/parquet.test.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: add Parquet encoding wrapper for Oura data tables\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 4: Rewrite oura.ts — Replace D1 writes with R2 Parquet\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `ingestion/src/services/oura.ts`\n\nThis is the core task. Replace the 4 `sync*` functions to write Parquet to R2 instead of D1.\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Add chunking utility**\n\nAdd to `oura.ts`:\n```typescript\nfunction splitIntoMonthlyChunks(startDate: string, endDate: string): Array<{ start: string; end: string }> {\n  const chunks: Array<{ start: string; end: string }> = [];\n  let current = new Date(startDate);\n  const end = new Date(endDate);\n\n  while (current < end) {\n    const chunkEnd = new Date(current);\n    chunkEnd.setMonth(chunkEnd.getMonth() + 1);\n    chunkEnd.setDate(0); // last day of current month\n    const effectiveEnd = chunkEnd > end ? end : chunkEnd;\n    chunks.push({\n      start: current.toISOString().split(\"T\")[0],\n      end: effectiveEnd.toISOString().split(\"T\")[0],\n    });\n    current = new Date(effectiveEnd);\n    current.setDate(current.getDate() + 1);\n  }\n  return chunks;\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Add R2 write helpers**\n\n```typescript\nfunction groupByDay<T extends { day?: string; timestamp?: string }>(items: T[]): Map<string, T[]> {\n  const map = new Map<string, T[]>();\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const day = item.day ?? item.timestamp?.split(\"T\")[0] ?? \"unknown\";\n    const arr = map.get(day) ?? [];\n    arr.push(item);\n    map.set(day, arr);\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\nasync function writeParquetToR2(\n  r2: R2Bucket,\n  tableName: string,\n  day: string,\n  data: Uint8Array\n): Promise<void> {\n  const key = `oura/${tableName}/${day}.parquet`;\n  await r2.put(key, data);\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite syncDailySleep to use R2**\n\nReplace D1 INSERT with:\n1. Fetch from Oura API (unchanged)\n2. Flatten contributors into top-level fields\n3. Group by day\n4. Encode each day's data to Parquet\n5. PUT to R2\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Rewrite syncDailyActivity, syncDailyReadiness, syncHeartRate similarly**\n\nSame pattern for all 4 tables. Heart rate uses `timestamp.split(\"T\")[0]` for day grouping.\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Rewrite runSync with chunking and backfill**\n\nReplace `runSync`:\n- Check `sync_state.last_sync_at` — if NULL, use `OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE`\n- Split date range into monthly chunks\n- For each chunk: refresh token if needed, fetch + encode + R2 PUT for all 4 tables\n- Add 1s delay between chunks\n- Update `sync_state` after each successful chunk\n- Pass `env.DATA_LAKE` (R2 bucket) instead of `env.DB` to sync functions\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Verify typecheck**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm typecheck\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: Run all tests**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm test:run\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 8: Lint**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm check:fix\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 9: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add ingestion/src/services/oura.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: replace D1 writes with R2 Parquet for Oura data [TA-398]\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 5: Final verification and cleanup\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Run full test suite**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm test:run\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run typecheck**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm typecheck\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Run lint**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm check\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Test local dev**\n\n```bash\ncd ingestion && pnpm dev\n```\n\nVerify worker starts without errors.\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Final commit if any remaining changes**\n"
}

/* tslint:disable */
/* eslint-disable */
/**
 * Read a Parquet file into Arrow data.
 *
 * This returns an Arrow table in WebAssembly memory. To transfer the Arrow table to JavaScript
 * memory you have two options:
 *
 * - (Easier): Call {@linkcode Table.intoIPCStream} to construct a buffer that can be parsed with
 *   Arrow JS's `tableFromIPC` function.
 * - (More performant but bleeding edge): Call {@linkcode Table.intoFFI} to construct a data
 *   representation that can be parsed zero-copy from WebAssembly with
 *   [arrow-js-ffi](https://github.com/kylebarron/arrow-js-ffi) using `parseTable`.
 *
 * Example with IPC stream:
 *
 * ```js
 * import { tableFromIPC } from "apache-arrow";
 * import initWasm, {readParquet} from "parquet-wasm";
 *
 * // Instantiate the WebAssembly context
 * await initWasm();
 *
 * const resp = await fetch("https://example.com/file.parquet");
 * const parquetUint8Array = new Uint8Array(await resp.arrayBuffer());
 * const arrowWasmTable = readParquet(parquetUint8Array);
 * const arrowTable = tableFromIPC(arrowWasmTable.intoIPCStream());
 * ```
 *
 * Example with `arrow-js-ffi`:
 *
 * ```js
 * import { parseTable } from "arrow-js-ffi";
 * import initWasm, {readParquet, wasmMemory} from "parquet-wasm";
 *
 * // Instantiate the WebAssembly context
 * await initWasm();
 * const WASM_MEMORY = wasmMemory();
 *
 * const resp = await fetch("https://example.com/file.parquet");
 * const parquetUint8Array = new Uint8Array(await resp.arrayBuffer());
 * const arrowWasmTable = readParquet(parquetUint8Array);
 * const ffiTable = arrowWasmTable.intoFFI();
 * const arrowTable = parseTable(
 *   WASM_MEMORY.buffer,
 *   ffiTable.arrayAddrs(),
 *   ffiTable.schemaAddr()
 * );
 * ```
 *
 * @param parquet_file Uint8Array containing Parquet data
 * @param options
 *
 *    Options for reading Parquet data. Optional keys include:
 *
 *    - `batchSize`: The number of rows in each batch. If not provided, the upstream parquet
 *           default is 1024.
 *    - `rowGroups`: Only read data from the provided row group indexes.
 *    - `limit`: Provide a limit to the number of rows to be read.
 *    - `offset`: Provide an offset to skip over the given number of rows.
 *    - `columns`: The column names from the file to read.
 */
export function readParquet(parquet_file: Uint8Array, options?: ReaderOptions | null): Table;
/**
 * Read an Arrow schema from a Parquet file in memory.
 *
 * This returns an Arrow schema in WebAssembly memory. To transfer the Arrow schema to JavaScript
 * memory you have two options:
 *
 * - (Easier): Call {@linkcode Schema.intoIPCStream} to construct a buffer that can be parsed with
 *   Arrow JS's `tableFromIPC` function. This results in an Arrow JS Table with zero rows but a
 *   valid schema.
 * - (More performant but bleeding edge): Call {@linkcode Schema.intoFFI} to construct a data
 *   representation that can be parsed zero-copy from WebAssembly with
 *   [arrow-js-ffi](https://github.com/kylebarron/arrow-js-ffi) using `parseSchema`.
 *
 * Example with IPC Stream:
 *
 * ```js
 * import { tableFromIPC } from "apache-arrow";
 * import initWasm, {readSchema} from "parquet-wasm";
 *
 * // Instantiate the WebAssembly context
 * await initWasm();
 *
 * const resp = await fetch("https://example.com/file.parquet");
 * const parquetUint8Array = new Uint8Array(await resp.arrayBuffer());
 * const arrowWasmSchema = readSchema(parquetUint8Array);
 * const arrowTable = tableFromIPC(arrowWasmSchema.intoIPCStream());
 * const arrowSchema = arrowTable.schema;
 * ```
 *
 * Example with `arrow-js-ffi`:
 *
 * ```js
 * import { parseSchema } from "arrow-js-ffi";
 * import initWasm, {readSchema, wasmMemory} from "parquet-wasm";
 *
 * // Instantiate the WebAssembly context
 * await initWasm();

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**parquet-wasm の書き込みパス**: `Table.fromIPCStream(arrowIPC) → writeParquet(table)` が基本パターン。Apache Arrow の IPC フォーマット（`tableToIPC` で生成）を中継して Parquet に変換する。つまり `apache-arrow` JS ライブラリも必要。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Apache Arrow JS も必要です。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Workers Vitest Pool の制限**: `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers` は Cloudflare Workers のランタイム (workerd) でテストを実行する。workerd は `file://` スキームの fetch をサポートしないため、`parquet-wasm` の WASM 初期化（内部で `fetch()` を使う）が失敗する。WASM バイナリを明示的に読み込んで `initWasm()` に渡すか、テスト環境を Node.js に変更する必要がある。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

workerd 環境で WASM のロードに問題があります。`initWasm()` に WASM バイナリを直接渡す方式に変更します。

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Workers Pool の WASM 制限**: `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers` は workerd ランタイムで実行する。workerd では `file://` fetch ができないため、parquet-wasm の自動初期化が動かない。さらに、`import` で `.wasm` を直接取り込む方式も vitest のバンドル経由では正しく解決されない可能性がある。テスト環境を Node.js にするか、WASM を `fs.readFileSync` で読み込む必要がある。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

parquet のテストは Workers pool ではなく Node.js 環境で実行する必要があります。vitest の `workspace` 機能で分離するか、テストファイル内で Node.js 向けの初期化をします。

シンプルに、parquet テストは Node.js 環境で別設定にします。

> TOOL

### Task 4: oura.ts を R2 Parquet に書き換え

これが最も重要な変更です。D1 の sync 関数を R2 Parquet 書き込みに変更し、月次チャンキングとバックフィルを実装します。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**設計上のポイント**: `initParquetWasm()` を Workers の起動時に呼ぶ必要がある。`runSync` の先頭で WASM モジュールを渡して初期化するのが自然。Workers では `.wasm` ファイルを `import` で静的に取り込めるが、この import は oura.ts 側（呼び出し元）で行い、parquet.ts は WASM 非依存に保つ。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

    75→
    76→/**

    78→ * In Workers: call with the imported .wasm module.
    79→ * In tests: call with the WASM bytes loaded via fs.readFileSync.
    80→ */
    81→export function initParquetWasm(wasmModule: BufferSource | WebAssembly.Module): void {
    82→  if (wasmInitialized) return;
    83→  initSync({ module: wasmModule });
    84→  wasmInitialized = true;
    85→}
    86→
    87→function ensureWasmInit(): void {
    88→  if (!wasmInitialized) {
    89→    throw new Error("parquet-wasm not initialized. Call initParquetWasm() first.");
    90→  }
    91→}
    92→
    93→// ============================================
    94→// Column extraction helpers
    95→// ============================================
    96→
    97→function extractColumn<T, K extends keyof T>(rows: T[], key: K): T[K][] {
    98→  return rows.map((r) => r[key]);
    99→}

   105→function arrowToParquet(schema: Schema, columns: Record<string, unknown[]>): Uint8Array {
   106→  const arrowTable = tableFromArrays(columns);
   107→  const ipcBytes = tableToIPC(arrowTable, "stream");
   108→  const wasmTable = WasmTable.fromIPCStream(ipcBytes);
   109→  return writeParquet(wasmTable);
   110→}
   111→
   112→export async function encodeDailySleep(rows: DailySleepRow[]): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   113→  ensureWasmInit();
   114→
   115→  const schema = new Schema([
   116→    new Field("day", new Utf8(), false),
   117→    new Field("score", new Int32(), true),
   118→    new Field("deep_sleep", new Int32(), true),
   119→    new Field("efficiency", new Int32(), true),
   120→    new Field("latency", new Int32(), true),
   121→    new Field("rem_sleep", new Int32(), true),
   122→    new Field("restfulness", new Int32(), true),
   123→    new Field("timing", new Int32(), true),
   124→    new Field("total_sleep", new Int32(), true),
   125→    new Field("timestamp", new Utf8(), false),
   126→    new Field("synced_at", new Utf8(), false),
   127→  ]);
   128→
   129→  return arrowToParquet(schema, {

   132→    deep_sleep: extractColumn(rows, "deep_sleep"),
   133→    efficiency: extractColumn(rows, "efficiency"),

   142→}
