  "content": "import { Hono } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Env, SyncResult } from \"../types\";\nimport {\n  initParquetWasm,\n  encodeDailySleep,\n  encodeDailyActivity,\n  encodeDailyReadiness,\n  encodeHeartRate,\n  type DailySleepRow,\n  type DailyActivityRow,\n  type DailyReadinessRow,\n  type HeartRateRow,\n} from \"./parquet\";\n// @ts-expect-error — Wrangler bundles .wasm as WebAssembly.Module\nimport PARQUET_WASM from \"../../node_modules/parquet-wasm/esm/parquet_wasm_bg.wasm\";\n\nconst app = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();\n\nconst OURA_AUTH_URL = \"https://cloud.ouraring.com/oauth/authorize\";\nconst OURA_TOKEN_URL = \"https://api.ouraring.com/oauth/token\";\nconst OURA_API_BASE = \"https://api.ouraring.com\";\n\n// ============================================\n// Types\n// ============================================\n\ninterface OuraTokenResponse {\n  access_token: string;\n  refresh_token: string;\n  token_type: string;\n  expires_in: number;\n  scope?: string;\n}\n\ninterface OuraResponse<T> {\n  data: T[];\n  next_token?: string;\n}\n\ninterface OuraDailySleep {\n  id: string;\n  day: string;\n  score: number | null;\n  timestamp: string;\n  contributors?: {\n    deep_sleep?: number | null;\n    efficiency?: number | null;\n    latency?: number | null;\n    rem_sleep?: number | null;\n    restfulness?: number | null;\n    timing?: number | null;\n    total_sleep?: number | null;\n  };\n}\n\ninterface OuraDailyActivity {\n  id: string;\n  day: string;\n  score: number | null;\n  active_calories: number;\n  total_calories: number;\n  steps: number;\n  equivalent_walking_distance: number;\n  high_activity_time: number;\n  medium_activity_time: number;\n  low_activity_time: number;\n  sedentary_time: number;\n  resting_time: number;\n  average_met_minutes?: number;\n  contributors?: {\n    meet_daily_targets?: number | null;\n    move_every_hour?: number | null;\n    recovery_time?: number | null;\n    stay_active?: number | null;\n    training_frequency?: number | null;\n    training_volume?: number | null;\n  };\n  timestamp: string;\n}\n\ninterface OuraDailyReadiness {\n  id: string;\n  day: string;\n  score: number | null;\n  temperature_deviation: number | null;\n  temperature_trend_deviation: number | null;\n  timestamp: string;\n  contributors?: {\n    activity_balance?: number | null;\n    body_temperature?: number | null;\n    hrv_balance?: number | null;\n    previous_day_activity?: number | null;\n    previous_night?: number | null;\n    recovery_index?: number | null;\n    resting_heart_rate?: number | null;\n    sleep_balance?: number | null;\n  };\n}\n\ninterface OuraHeartRate {\n  bpm: number;\n  source: string;\n  timestamp: string;\n}\n\n// ============================================\n// OAuth2\n// ============================================\n\nasync function getValidToken(db: D1Database, env: Env): Promise<string> {\n  const row = await db\n    .prepare(\"SELECT access_token, refresh_token, expires_at FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura'\")\n    .first<{ access_token: string; refresh_token: string; expires_at: string }>();\n\n  if (!row) {\n    throw new Error(\"Oura not authenticated. Visit /oura/auth to connect.\");\n  }\n\n  const now = new Date();\n  const expiresAt = new Date(row.expires_at);\n\n  if (now < expiresAt) {\n    return row.access_token;\n  }\n\n  // Refresh token\n  const res = await fetch(OURA_TOKEN_URL, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\" },\n    body: new URLSearchParams({\n      grant_type: \"refresh_token\",\n      refresh_token: row.refresh_token,\n      client_id: env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,\n      client_secret: env.OURA_CLIENT_SECRET,\n    }),\n  });\n\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const text = await res.text();\n    throw new Error(`Oura token refresh failed: ${res.status} ${text}`);\n  }\n\n  const token=[REDACTED] = await res.json();\n  const newExpiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + token.expires_in * 1000).toISOString();\n\n  await db\n    .prepare(\n      `UPDATE oauth_tokens SET\n        access_token = ?, refresh_token = ?, expires_at = ?, updated_at = datetime('now')\n       WHERE id = 'oura'`\n    )\n    .bind(token.access_token, token.refresh_token, newExpiresAt)\n    .run();\n\n  return token.access_token;\n}\n\n// ============================================\n// API Client\n// ============================================\n\nasync function fetchOura<T>(\n  path: string,\n  token: string,\n  params?: Record<string, string>\n): Promise<OuraResponse<T>> {\n  const url = new URL(`${OURA_API_BASE}${path}`);\n  if (params) {\n    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {\n      url.searchParams.set(k, v);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const res = await fetch(url.toString(), {\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,\n      \"User-Agent\": \"ingestion-worker\",\n    },\n  });\n\n  if (res.status === 429) {\n    throw new Error(\"Oura API rate limit exceeded\");\n  }\n  if (res.status === 401) {\n    throw new Error(\"Oura API unauthorized - token may be expired\");\n  }\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const body = await res.text();\n    const truncated = body.length > 500 ? `${body.slice(0, 500)}...(truncated)` : body;\n    throw new Error(`Oura API error: ${res.status} - ${truncated || \"no response body\"}`);\n  }\n\n  return res.json();\n}\n\nasync function fetchAllPages<T>(\n  path: string,\n  token: string,\n  params: Record<string, string>\n): Promise<T[]> {\n  const allData: T[] = [];\n  let nextToken: string | undefined;\n\n  do {\n    const reqParams = { ...params };\n    if (nextToken) {\n      reqParams.next_token = nextToken;\n    }\n\n    const response = await fetchOura<T>(path, token, reqParams);\n    allData.push(...response.data);\n    nextToken = response.next_token;\n  } while (nextToken);\n\n  return allData;\n}\n\n// ============================================\n// Chunking & Grouping Utilities\n// ============================================\n\nfunction splitIntoMonthlyChunks(\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string\n): Array<{ start: string; end: string }> {\n  const chunks: Array<{ start: string; end: string }> = [];\n  let current = new Date(`${startDate}T00:00:00Z`);\n  const end = new Date(`${endDate}T00:00:00Z`);\n\n  while (current <= end) {\n    const chunkEnd = new Date(current);\n    chunkEnd.setUTCMonth(chunkEnd.getUTCMonth() + 1);\n    chunkEnd.setUTCDate(0); // last day of current month\n    const effectiveEnd = chunkEnd > end ? end : chunkEnd;\n    chunks.push({\n      start: current.toISOString().split(\"T\")[0],\n      end: effectiveEnd.toISOString().split(\"T\")[0],\n    });\n    current = new Date(effectiveEnd);\n    current.setUTCDate(current.getUTCDate() + 1);\n  }\n  return chunks;\n}\n\nfunction groupByDay<T>(items: T[], getDayFn: (item: T) => string): Map<string, T[]> {\n  const map = new Map<string, T[]>();\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const day = getDayFn(item);\n    const arr = map.get(day) ?? [];\n    arr.push(item);\n    map.set(day, arr);\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\nasync function writeParquetToR2(\n  r2: R2Bucket,\n  tableName: string,\n  day: string,\n  data: Uint8Array\n): Promise<void> {\n  const key = `oura/${tableName}/${day}.parquet`;\n  await r2.put(key, data);\n}\n\nfunction sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {\n  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));\n}\n\n// ============================================\n// Sync Functions (R2 Parquet)\n// ============================================\n\nasync function syncDailySleep(\n  r2: R2Bucket,\n  token: string,\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraDailySleep>(\"/v2/usercollection/daily_sleep\", token, {\n    start_date: startDate,\n    end_date: endDate,\n  });\n\n  if (data.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  const synced_at = new Date().toISOString();\n  const grouped = groupByDay(data, (item) => item.day);\n\n  for (const [day, items] of grouped) {\n    const rows: DailySleepRow[] = items.map((item) => ({\n      day: item.day,\n      score: item.score ?? null,\n      deep_sleep: item.contributors?.deep_sleep ?? null,\n      efficiency: item.contributors?.efficiency ?? null,\n      latency: item.contributors?.latency ?? null,\n      rem_sleep: item.contributors?.rem_sleep ?? null,\n      restfulness: item.contributors?.restfulness ?? null,\n      timing: item.contributors?.timing ?? null,\n      total_sleep: item.contributors?.total_sleep ?? null,\n      timestamp: item.timestamp,\n      synced_at,\n    }));\n    const parquet = await encodeDailySleep(rows);\n    await writeParquetToR2(r2, \"daily_sleep\", day, parquet);\n  }\n\n  return data.length;\n}\n\nasync function syncDailyActivity(\n  r2: R2Bucket,\n  token: string,\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraDailyActivity>(\"/v2/usercollection/daily_activity\", token, {\n    start_date: startDate,\n    end_date: endDate,\n  });\n\n  if (data.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  const synced_at = new Date().toISOString();\n  const grouped = groupByDay(data, (item) => item.day);\n\n  for (const [day, items] of grouped) {\n    const rows: DailyActivityRow[] = items.map((item) => ({\n      day: item.day,\n      score: item.score ?? null,\n      active_calories: item.active_calories,\n      total_calories: item.total_calories,\n      steps: item.steps,\n      equivalent_walking_distance: item.equivalent_walking_distance,\n      high_activity_time: item.high_activity_time,\n      medium_activity_time: item.medium_activity_time,\n      low_activity_time: item.low_activity_time,\n      sedentary_time: item.sedentary_time,\n      resting_time: item.resting_time,\n      met_average: item.average_met_minutes ?? null,\n      meet_daily_targets: item.contributors?.meet_daily_targets ?? null,\n      move_every_hour: item.contributors?.move_every_hour ?? null,\n      recovery_time: item.contributors?.recovery_time ?? null,\n      stay_active: item.contributors?.stay_active ?? null,\n      training_frequency: item.contributors?.training_frequency ?? null,\n      training_volume: item.contributors?.training_volume ?? null,\n      timestamp: item.timestamp,\n      synced_at,\n    }));\n    const parquet = await encodeDailyActivity(rows);\n    await writeParquetToR2(r2, \"daily_activity\", day, parquet);\n  }\n\n  return data.length;\n}\n\nasync function syncDailyReadiness(\n  r2: R2Bucket,\n  token: string,\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraDailyReadiness>(\n    \"/v2/usercollection/daily_readiness\",\n    token,\n    { start_date: startDate, end_date: endDate }\n  );\n\n  if (data.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  const synced_at = new Date().toISOString();\n  const grouped = groupByDay(data, (item) => item.day);\n\n  for (const [day, items] of grouped) {\n    const rows: DailyReadinessRow[] = items.map((item) => ({\n      day: item.day,\n      score: item.score ?? null,\n      temperature_deviation: item.temperature_deviation ?? null,\n      temperature_trend_deviation: item.temperature_trend_deviation ?? null,\n      activity_balance: item.contributors?.activity_balance ?? null,\n      body_temperature: item.contributors?.body_temperature ?? null,\n      hrv_balance: item.contributors?.hrv_balance ?? null,\n      previous_day_activity: item.contributors?.previous_day_activity ?? null,\n      previous_night: item.contributors?.previous_night ?? null,\n      recovery_index: item.contributors?.recovery_index ?? null,\n      resting_heart_rate: item.contributors?.resting_heart_rate ?? null,\n      sleep_balance: item.contributors?.sleep_balance ?? null,\n      timestamp: item.timestamp,\n      synced_at,\n    }));\n    const parquet = await encodeDailyReadiness(rows);\n    await writeParquetToR2(r2, \"daily_readiness\", day, parquet);\n  }\n\n  return data.length;\n}\n\nasync function syncHeartRate(\n  r2: R2Bucket,\n  token: string,\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  // Heart rate API uses datetime params and has a 30-day limit\n  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraHeartRate>(\"/v2/usercollection/heartrate\", token, {\n    start_datetime: `${startDate}T00:00:00+00:00`,\n    end_datetime: `${endDate}T23:59:59+00:00`,\n  });\n\n  if (data.length === 0) return 0;\n\n  const synced_at = new Date().toISOString();\n  const grouped = groupByDay(data, (item) => item.timestamp.split(\"T\")[0]);\n\n  for (const [day, items] of grouped) {\n    const rows: HeartRateRow[] = items.map((item) => ({\n      bpm: item.bpm,\n      source: item.source,\n      timestamp: item.timestamp,\n      day,\n      synced_at,\n    }));\n    const parquet = await encodeHeartRate(rows);\n    await writeParquetToR2(r2, \"heart_rate\", day, parquet);\n  }\n\n  return data.length;\n}\n\n// ============================================\n// Main Sync\n// ============================================\n\nexport async function runSync(env: Env): Promise<SyncResult> {\n  const db = env.DB;\n  const r2 = env.DATA_LAKE;\n\n  // Initialize parquet-wasm\n  initParquetWasm(PARQUET_WASM);\n\n  // Get valid OAuth token (refreshes if expired)\n  let token = await getValidToken(db, env);\n\n  // Determine date range\n  const syncState = await db\n    .prepare(\"SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura'\")\n    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();\n\n  const lastSyncAt = syncState?.last_sync_at ?? null;\n  const today = new Date().toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n\n  let startDate: string;\n  if (!lastSyncAt) {\n    // First sync: use backfill start date\n    startDate = env.OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE || \"2024-01-01\";\n    console.log(`Oura backfill from ${startDate} to ${today}`);\n  } else {\n    // Incremental: from last sync - 1 day (overlap for idempotent overwrites)\n    const start = new Date(lastSyncAt);\n    start.setDate(start.getDate() - 1);\n    startDate = start.toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n  }\n\n  // Split into monthly chunks\n  const chunks = splitIntoMonthlyChunks(startDate, today);\n  console.log(`Oura sync: ${chunks.length} monthly chunk(s) from ${startDate} to ${today}`);\n\n  let totalSleep = 0;\n  let totalActivity = 0;\n  let totalReadiness = 0;\n  let totalHeartRate = 0;\n  let lastSuccessfulEnd = startDate;\n\n  for (const chunk of chunks) {\n    try {\n      // Refresh token if needed before each chunk\n      token = await getValidToken(db, env);\n\n      const sleepCount = await syncDailySleep(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);\n      const activityCount = await syncDailyActivity(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);\n      const readinessCount = await syncDailyReadiness(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);\n\n      // Heart rate: limit to 30 days per chunk\n      const chunkDays =\n        (new Date(chunk.end).getTime() - new Date(chunk.start).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);\n      const heartRateCount =\n        chunkDays > 30 ? 0 : await syncHeartRate(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);\n\n      totalSleep += sleepCount;\n      totalActivity += activityCount;\n      totalReadiness += readinessCount;\n      totalHeartRate += heartRateCount;\n      lastSuccessfulEnd = chunk.end;\n\n      console.log(\n        `Oura chunk ${chunk.start}~${chunk.end}: ${sleepCount} sleep, ${activityCount} activity, ${readinessCount} readiness, ${heartRateCount} HR`\n      );\n\n      // Rate limit: 1s delay between chunks\n      if (chunks.indexOf(chunk) < chunks.length - 1) {\n        await sleep(1000);\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : \"Unknown error\";\n      console.error(`Oura chunk ${chunk.start}~${chunk.end} failed: ${message}`);\n      if (message.includes(\"rate limit\")) {\n        // Skip remaining chunks on rate limit\n        break;\n      }\n      // Other errors: skip this chunk and continue\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Update sync_state with last successful chunk end\n  await db\n    .prepare(\n      \"UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = 'oura'\"\n    )\n    .bind(lastSuccessfulEnd)\n    .run();\n\n  const total = totalSleep + totalActivity + totalReadiness + totalHeartRate;\n\n  return {\n    service: \"oura\",\n    success: true,\n    message: `Synced ${totalSleep} sleep, ${totalActivity} activity, ${totalReadiness} readiness, ${totalHeartRate} heart rate records to R2 Parquet`,\n    count: total,\n  };\n}\n\n// ============================================\n// Routes\n// ============================================\n\n// OAuth2 authorization\napp.get(\"/auth\", async (c) => {\n  const state = crypto.randomUUID();\n\n  // Persist state for CSRF validation (expires in 10 minutes)\n  const stateExpiry = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();\n  await c.env.DB.prepare(\n    `INSERT INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at)\n     VALUES ('oura_state', ?, '', ?)\n     ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET access_token = excluded.access_token, expires_at = excluded.expires_at`\n  )\n    .bind(state, stateExpiry)\n    .run();\n\n  const params = new URLSearchParams({\n    response_type: \"code\",\n    client_id: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,\n    redirect_uri: c.env.OURA_REDIRECT_URI,\n    scope: \"daily heartrate\",\n    state,\n  });\n  return c.redirect(`${OURA_AUTH_URL}?${params.toString()}`);\n});\n\n// OAuth2 callback\napp.get(\"/callback\", async (c) => {\n  const code = c.req.query(\"code\");\n  const state = c.req.query(\"state\");\n  if (!code) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing authorization code\" }, 400);\n  }\n\n  // Validate state for CSRF protection\n  const storedState = await c.env.DB.prepare(\n    \"SELECT access_token, expires_at FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura_state'\"\n  ).first<{ access_token: string; expires_at: string }>();\n\n  await c.env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura_state'\").run();\n\n  if (!storedState || storedState.access_token !== state) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid state parameter\" }, 400);\n  }\n  if (new Date(storedState.expires_at) < new Date()) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"State expired, please retry authorization\" }, 400);\n  }\n\n  const res = await fetch(OURA_TOKEN_URL, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\" },\n    body: new URLSearchParams({\n      grant_type: \"authorization_code\",\n      code,\n      client_id: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,\n      client_secret: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_SECRET,\n      redirect_uri: c.env.OURA_REDIRECT_URI,\n    }),\n  });\n\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const text = await res.text();\n    return c.json({ error: \"Token exchange failed\", detail: text }, 500);\n  }\n\n  const token=[REDACTED] = await res.json();\n  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + token.expires_in * 1000).toISOString();\n\n  await c.env.DB.prepare(\n    `INSERT INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, token_type, expires_at, scope, updated_at)\n     VALUES ('oura', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))\n     ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET\n       access_token = excluded.access_token,\n       refresh_token = excluded.refresh_token,\n       token_type = excluded.token_type,\n       expires_at = excluded.expires_at,\n       scope = excluded.scope,\n       updated_at = excluded.updated_at`\n  )\n    .bind(token.access_token, token.refresh_token, token.token_type, expiresAt, token.scope ?? null)\n    .run();\n\n  return c.json({ success: true, message: \"Oura connected successfully\" });\n});\n\n// Manual sync\napp.post(\"/sync\", async (c) => {\n  try {\n    const result = await runSync(c.env);\n    return c.json(result);\n  } catch (e) {\n    const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : \"Unknown error\";\n    const status = message.includes(\"not authenticated\") ? 401 : 500;\n    return c.json({ service: \"oura\", success: false, message }, status);\n  }\n});\n\n// Stats — list R2 objects count\napp.get(\"/stats\", async (c) => {\n  const tables = [\"daily_sleep\", \"daily_activity\", \"daily_readiness\", \"heart_rate\"];\n  const stats: Record<string, number> = {};\n\n  for (const table of tables) {\n    const list = await c.env.DATA_LAKE.list({ prefix: `oura/${table}/` });\n    stats[`${table}_files`] = list.objects.length;\n  }\n\n  const syncState = await c.env.DB.prepare(\n    \"SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura'\"\n  ).first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();\n\n  return c.json({ ...stats, last_sync: syncState?.last_sync_at ?? null });\n});\n\nexport default app;\n"
}

    81→export function initParquetWasm(wasmModule: BufferSource | WebAssembly.Module): void {

\"timestamp\"),\n    synced_at: extractColumn(rows, \"synced_at\"),\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function encodeDailyReadiness(rows: DailyReadinessRow[]): Promise<Uint8Array> {\n  ensureWasmInit();\n  return arrowToParquet({\n    day: extractColumn(rows, \"day\"),\n    score: extractColumn(rows, \"score\"),\n    temperature_deviation: extractColumn(rows, \"temperature_deviation\"),\n    temperature_trend_deviation: extractColumn(rows, \"temperature_trend_deviation\"),\n    activity_balance: extractColumn(rows, \"activity_balance\"),\n    body_temperature: extractColumn(rows,      1→import { env, fetchMock, SELF } from "cloudflare:test";
     2→import { afterEach, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     3→
     4→const OURA_MIGRATION_SQL = `
     5→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oauth_tokens (
     6→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
     7→    access_token TEXT NOT NULL,
     8→    refresh_token TEXT NOT NULL,
     9→    token_type TEXT DEFAULT 'Bearer',
    10→    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    11→    scope TEXT,
    12→    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    13→    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    14→);
    15→
    16→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_sleep (
    17→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    18→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    19→    score INTEGER,
    20→    timestamp TEXT,
    21→    deep_sleep INTEGER,
    22→    efficiency INTEGER,
    23→    latency INTEGER,
    24→    rem_sleep INTEGER,
    25→    restfulness INTEGER,
    26→    timing INTEGER,
    27→    total_sleep INTEGER,
    28→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    29→);
    30→
    31→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_activity (
    32→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    33→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    34→    score INTEGER,
    35→    active_calories INTEGER,
    36→    total_calories INTEGER,
    37→    steps INTEGER,
    38→    equivalent_walking_distance REAL,
    39→    high_activity_time INTEGER,
    40→    medium_activity_time INTEGER,
    41→    low_activity_time INTEGER,
    42→    sedentary_time INTEGER,
    43→    resting_time INTEGER,
    44→    met_average REAL,
    45→    meet_daily_targets INTEGER,
    46→    move_every_hour INTEGER,
    47→    recovery_time INTEGER,
    48→    stay_active INTEGER,
    49→    training_frequency INTEGER,
    50→    training_volume INTEGER,
    51→    timestamp TEXT,
    52→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    53→);
    54→
    55→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_daily_readiness (
    56→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    57→    day TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    58→    score INTEGER,
    59→    temperature_deviation REAL,
    60→    temperature_trend_deviation REAL,
    61→    timestamp TEXT,
    62→    activity_balance INTEGER,
    63→    body_temperature INTEGER,
    64→    hrv_balance INTEGER,
    65→    previous_day_activity INTEGER,
    66→    previous_night INTEGER,
    67→    recovery_index INTEGER,
    68→    resting_heart_rate INTEGER,
    69→    sleep_balance INTEGER,
    70→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    71→);
    72→
    73→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oura_heart_rate (
    74→    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    75→    bpm INTEGER NOT NULL,
    76→    source TEXT,
    77→    timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
    78→    day TEXT NOT NULL,
    79→    synced_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
    80→);
    81→
    82→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_sleep_day ON oura_daily_sleep(day DESC);
    83→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_activity_day ON oura_daily_activity(day DESC);
    84→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_daily_readiness_day ON oura_daily_readiness(day DESC);
    85→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_day ON oura_heart_rate(day DESC);
    86→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oura_heart_rate_timestamp ON oura_heart_rate(timestamp DESC);
    87→
    88→CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS v_oura_daily_summary AS
    89→SELECT
    90→    s.day,
    91→    s.score AS sleep_score,
    92→    a.score AS activity_score,
    93→    r.score AS readiness_score,
    94→    a.steps,
    95→    a.total_calories,
    96→    a.active_calories,
    97→    r.temperature_deviation,
    98→    s.deep_sleep AS sleep_deep_sleep,
    99→    s.efficiency AS sleep_efficiency,
   100→    s.total_sleep AS sleep_total_sleep
   101→FROM oura_daily_sleep s
   102→LEFT JOIN oura_daily_activity a ON s.day = a.day
   103→LEFT JOIN oura_daily_readiness r ON s.day = r.day
   104→ORDER BY s.day DESC;
   105→
   106→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS data_sources (
   107→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   108→    name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
   109→    source_type TEXT NOT NULL,
   110→    api_endpoint TEXT,
   111→    schedule_cron TEXT,
   112→    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
   113→    config_json TEXT,
   114→    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
   115→    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
   116→);
   117→
   118→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sync_state (
   119→    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   120→    data_source_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   121→    last_sync_at TEXT,
   122→    last_cursor TEXT,
   123→    metadata_json TEXT,
   124→    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
   125→    FOREIGN KEY (data_source_id) REFERENCES data_sources(id)
   126→);
   127→
   128→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO data_sources (id, name, source_type, api_endpoint, schedule_cron)
   129→VALUES ('oura', 'Oura Ring', 'api', 'https://api.ouraring.com', '0 0 * * *');
   130→
   131→INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_state (id, data_source_id, last_sync_at)
   132→VALUES ('oura', 'oura', NULL);
   133→`;
   134→
   135→async function insertTestToken(expiresAt?: string) {
   136→  const expires = expiresAt ?? new Date(Date.now() + 3600 * 1000).toISOString();
   137→  await env.DB.prepare(
   138→    `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at)
   139→     VALUES ('oura', 'test-access-token', 'test-refresh-token', ?)`
   140→  )
   141→    .bind(expires)
   142→    .run();
   143→}
   144→
   145→beforeAll(async () => {
   146→  const statements = OURA_MIGRATION_SQL.split(";")
   147→    .map((s) => s.trim())
   148→    .filter((s) => s.length > 0);
   149→
   150→  for (const sql of statements) {
   151→    await env.DB.prepare(sql).run();
   152→  }
   153→
   154→  fetchMock.activate();
   155→  fetchMock.disableNetConnect();
   156→});
   157→
   158→afterEach(() => {
   159→  fetchMock.assertNoPendingInterceptors();
   160→});
   161→
   162→describe("oura oauth2", () => {
   163→  it("redirects to Oura auth page on GET /oura/auth", async () => {
   164→    const res = await SELF.fetch("https://example.com/oura/auth", { redirect: "manual" });
   165→    expect(res.status).toBe(302);
   166→
   167→    const location = res.headers.get("Location") ?? "";
   168→    expect(location).toContain("cloud.ouraring.com/oauth/authorize");
   169→    expect(location).toContain("client_id=");
   170→    expect(location).toContain("redirect_uri=");
   171→    expect(location).toContain("scope=daily+heartrate");
   172→  });
   173→
   174→  it("returns error when callback has no code", async () => {
   175→    const res = await SELF.fetch("https://example.com/oura/callback");
   176→    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
   177→
   178→    const json = await res.json();
   179→    expect(json).toHaveProperty("error", "Missing authorization code");
   180→  });
   181→
   182→  it("exchanges code for token on callback with valid state", async () => {
   183→    // Pre-store state for CSRF validation
   184→    const futureExpiry = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
   185→    await env.DB.prepare(
   186→      `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at)
   187→       VALUES ('oura_state', 'test-state-value', '', ?)`
   188→    )
   189→      .bind(futureExpiry)
   190→      .run();
   191→
   192→    fetchMock
   193→      .get("https://api.ouraring.com")
   194→      .intercept({ path: "/oauth/token", method: "POST" })
   195→      .reply(200, {
   196→        access_token=[REDACTED]",
   197→        refresh_token=[REDACTED]",
   198→        token_type: "Bearer",
   199→        expires_in: 86400,
   200→        scope: "daily heartrate",
   201→      });
   202→
   203→    const res = await SELF.fetch(
   204→      "https://example.com/oura/callback?code=test-auth-code&state=test-state-value"
   205→    );
   206→    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
   207→
   208→    const json = await res.json();
   209→    expect(json).toHaveProperty("success", true);
   210→
   211→    // Verify token was saved
   212→    const token = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura'").first();
   213→    expect(token).not.toBeNull();
   214→    expect(token?.access_token).toBe("new-access-token");
   215→  });
   216→
   217→  it("rejects callback with invalid state", async () => {
   218→    const futureExpiry = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
   219→    await env.DB.prepare(
   220→      `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at)
   221→       VALUES ('oura_state', 'correct-state', '', ?)`
   222→    )
   223→      .bind(futureExpiry)
   224→      .run();
   225→
   226→    const res = await SELF.fetch(
   227→      "https://example.com/oura/callback?code=test-auth-code&state=wrong-state"
   228→    );
   229→    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
   230→
   231→    const json = await res.json();
   232→    expect(json).toHaveProperty("error", "Invalid state parameter");
   233→  });
   234→});

   106→// ============================================
   107→// OAuth2
   108→// ============================================
   109→
   110→async function getValidToken(db: D1Database, env: Env): Promise<string> {
   111→  const row = await db
   112→    .prepare("SELECT access_token, refresh_token, expires_at FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura'")
   113→    .first<{ access_token: string; refresh_token: string; expires_at: string }>();
   114→
   115→  if (!row) {
   116→    throw new Error("Oura not authenticated. Visit /oura/auth to connect.");
   117→  }
   118→
   119→  const now = new Date();
   120→  const expiresAt = new Date(row.expires_at);
   121→
   122→  if (now < expiresAt) {
   123→    return row.access_token;
   124→  }
   125→
   126→  // Refresh token
   127→  const res = await fetch(OURA_TOKEN_URL, {
   128→    method: "POST",
   129→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
   130→    body: new URLSearchParams({
   131→      grant_type: "refresh_token",
   132→      refresh_token: row.refresh_token,
   133→      client_id: env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,
   134→      client_secret: env.OURA_CLIENT_SECRET,
   135→    }),
   136→  });
   137→
   138→  if (!res.ok) {
   139→    const text = await res.text();
   140→    throw new Error(`Oura token refresh failed: ${res.status} ${text}`);
   141→  }
   142→
   143→  const token=[REDACTED] = await res.json();
   144→  const newExpiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + token.expires_in * 1000).toISOString();
   145→
   146→  await db
   147→    .prepare(
   148→      `UPDATE oauth_tokens SET
   149→        access_token = ?, refresh_token = ?, expires_at = ?, updated_at = datetime('now')
   150→       WHERE id = 'oura'`
   151→    )
   152→    .bind(token.access_token, token.refresh_token, newExpiresAt)
   153→    .run();
   154→
   155→  return token.access_token;
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ============================================
   159→// API Client
   160→// ============================================
   161→
   162→async function fetchOura<T>(
   163→  path: string,
   164→  token: string,
   165→  params?: Record<string, string>
   166→): Promise<OuraResponse<T>> {
   167→  const url = new URL(`${OURA_API_BASE}${path}`);
   168→  if (params) {
   169→    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
   170→      url.searchParams.set(k, v);
   171→    }
   172→  }
   173→
   174→  const res = await fetch(url.toString(), {
   175→    headers: {
   176→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
   177→      "User-Agent": "ingestion-worker",
   178→    },
   179→  });
   180→
   181→  if (res.status === 429) {
   182→    throw new Error("Oura API rate limit exceeded");
   183→  }
   184→  if (res.status === 401) {
   185→    throw new Error("Oura API unauthorized - token may be expired");
   186→  }
   187→  if (!res.ok) {
   188→    const body = await res.text();
   189→    const truncated = body.length > 500 ? `${body.slice(0, 500)}...(truncated)` : body;
   190→    throw new Error(`Oura API error: ${res.status} - ${truncated || "no response body"}`);
   191→  }
   192→
   193→  return res.json();
   194→}
   195→
   196→async function fetchAllPages<T>(
   197→  path: string,
   198→  token: string,
   199→  params: Record<string, string>
   200→): Promise<T[]> {
   201→  const allData: T[] = [];
   202→  let nextToken: string | undefined;
   203→
   204→  do {
   205→    const reqParams = { ...params };
   206→    if (nextToken) {
   207→      reqParams.next_token = nextToken;
   208→    }
   209→
   210→    const response = await fetchOura<T>(path, token, reqParams);
   211→    allData.push(...response.data);
   212→    nextToken = response.next_token;
   213→  } while (nextToken);
   214→
   215→  return allData;
   216→}
   217→
   218→// ============================================
   219→// Chunking & Grouping Utilities
   220→// ============================================
   221→
   222→function splitIntoMonthlyChunks(
   223→  startDate: string,
   224→  endDate: string
   225→): Array<{ start: string; end: string }> {
   226→  const chunks: Array<{ start: string; end: string }> = [];
   227→  let current = new Date(`${startDate}T00:00:00Z`);
   228→  const end = new Date(`${endDate}T00:00:00Z`);
   229→
   230→  while (current <= end) {
   231→    const chunkEnd = new Date(current);
   232→    chunkEnd.setUTCMonth(chunkEnd.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   233→    chunkEnd.setUTCDate(0); // last day of current month
   234→    const effectiveEnd = chunkEnd > end ? end : chunkEnd;
   235→    chunks.push({
   236→      start: current.toISOString().split("T")[0],
   237→      end: effectiveEnd.toISOString().split("T")[0],
   238→    });
   239→    current = new Date(effectiveEnd);
   240→    current.setUTCDate(current.getUTCDate() + 1);
   241→  }
   242→  return chunks;
   243→}
   244→
   245→function groupByDay<T>(items: T[], getDayFn: (item: T) => string): Map<string, T[]> {
   246→  const map = new Map<string, T[]>();
   247→  for (const item of items) {
   248→    const day = getDayFn(item);
   249→    const arr = map.get(day) ?? [];
   250→    arr.push(item);
   251→    map.set(day, arr);
   252→  }
   253→  return map;
   254→}
   255→
   256→async function writeParquetToR2(
   257→  r2: R2Bucket,
   258→  tableName: string,
   259→  day: string,
   260→  data: Uint8Array
   261→): Promise<void> {
   262→  const key = `oura/${tableName}/${day}.parquet`;
   263→  await r2.put(key, data);
   264→}
   265→
   266→function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
   267→  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
   268→}
   269→
   270→// ============================================
   271→// Sync Functions (R2 Parquet)
   272→// ============================================
   273→
   274→async function syncDailySleep(
   275→  r2: R2Bucket,
   276→  token: string,
   277→  startDate: string,
   278→  endDate: string
   279→): Promise<number> {
   280→  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraDailySleep>("/v2/usercollection/daily_sleep", token, {
   281→    start_date: startDate,
   282→    end_date: endDate,
   283→  });
   284→
   285→  if (data.length === 0) return 0;
   286→
   287→  const synced_at = new Date().toISOString();
   288→  const grouped = groupByDay(data, (item) => item.day);
   289→
   290→  for (const [day, items] of grouped) {
   291→    const rows: DailySleepRow[] = items.map((item) => ({
   292→      day: item.day,
   293→      score: item.score ?? null,
   294→      deep_sleep: item.contributors?.deep_sleep ?? null,
   295→      efficiency: item.contributors?.efficiency ?? null,
   296→      latency: item.contributors?.latency ?? null,
   297→      rem_sleep: item.contributors?.rem_sleep ?? null,
   298→      restfulness: item.contributors?.restfulness ?? null,
   299→      timing: item.contributors?.timing ?? null,
   300→      total_sleep: item.contributors?.total_sleep ?? null,
   301→      timestamp: item.timestamp,
   302→      synced_at,
   303→    }));
   304→    const parquet = await encodeDailySleep(rows);
   305→    await writeParquetToR2(r2, "daily_sleep", day, parquet);
   306→  }
   307→
   308→  return data.length;
   309→}

   398→async function syncHeartRate(
   399→  r2: R2Bucket,
   400→  token: string,
   401→  startDate: string,
   402→  endDate: string
   403→): Promise<number> {
   404→  // Heart rate API uses datetime params and has a 30-day limit
   405→  const data = await fetchAllPages<OuraHeartRate>("/v2/usercollection/heartrate", token, {
   406→    start_datetime: `${startDate}T00:00:00+00:00`,
   407→    end_datetime: `${endDate}T23:59:59+00:00`,
   408→  });
   409→
   410→  if (data.length === 0) return 0;
   411→
   412→  const synced_at = new Date().toISOString();
   413→  const grouped = groupByDay(data, (item) => item.timestamp.split("T")[0]);
   414→
   415→  for (const [day, items] of grouped) {
   416→    const rows: HeartRateRow[] = items.map((item) => ({
   417→      bpm: item.bpm,
   418→      source: item.source,
   419→      timestamp: item.timestamp,
   420→      day,
   421→      synced_at,
   422→    }));
   423→    const parquet = await encodeHeartRate(rows);
   424→    await writeParquetToR2(r2, "heart_rate", day, parquet);
   425→  }
   426→
   427→  return data.length;
   428→}
   429→
   430→// ============================================
   431→// Main Sync
   432→// ============================================
   433→
   434→export async function runSync(env: Env): Promise<SyncResult> {
   435→  const db = env.DB;
   436→  const r2 = env.DATA_LAKE;
   437→
   438→  // Initialize parquet-wasm
   439→  initParquetWasm(PARQUET_WASM);
   440→
   441→  // Get valid OAuth token (refreshes if expired)
   442→  let token = await getValidToken(db, env);
   443→
   444→  // Determine date range
   445→  const syncState = await db
   446→    .prepare("SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura'")
   447→    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();
   448→
   449→  const lastSyncAt = syncState?.last_sync_at ?? null;
   450→  const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
   451→
   452→  let startDate: string;
   453→  if (!lastSyncAt) {
   454→    // First sync: use backfill start date
   455→    startDate = env.OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE || "2024-01-01";
   456→    console.log(`Oura backfill from ${startDate} to ${today}`);
   457→  } else {
   458→    // Incremental: from last sync - 1 day (overlap for idempotent overwrites)
   459→    const start = new Date(lastSyncAt);
   460→    start.setDate(start.getDate() - 1);
   461→    startDate = start.toISOString().split("T")[0];
   462→  }
   463→
   464→  // Split into monthly chunks
   465→  const chunks = splitIntoMonthlyChunks(startDate, today);
   466→  console.log(`Oura sync: ${chunks.length} monthly chunk(s) from ${startDate} to ${today}`);
   467→
   468→  let totalSleep = 0;
   469→  let totalActivity = 0;
   470→  let totalReadiness = 0;
   471→  let totalHeartRate = 0;
   472→  let lastSuccessfulEnd = startDate;
   473→
   474→  for (const chunk of chunks) {
   475→    try {
   476→      // Refresh token if needed before each chunk
   477→      token = await getValidToken(db, env);
   478→
   479→      const sleepCount = await syncDailySleep(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);
   480→      const activityCount = await syncDailyActivity(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);
   481→      const readinessCount = await syncDailyReadiness(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);
   482→
   483→      // Heart rate: limit to 30 days per chunk
   484→      const chunkDays =
   485→        (new Date(chunk.end).getTime() - new Date(chunk.start).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
   486→      const heartRateCount =
   487→        chunkDays > 30 ? 0 : await syncHeartRate(r2, token, chunk.start, chunk.end);
   488→
   489→      totalSleep += sleepCount;
   490→      totalActivity += activityCount;
   491→      totalReadiness += readinessCount;
   492→      totalHeartRate += heartRateCount;
   493→      lastSuccessfulEnd = chunk.end;
   494→
   495→      console.log(
   496→        `Oura chunk ${chunk.start}~${chunk.end}: ${sleepCount} sleep, ${activityCount} activity, ${readinessCount} readiness, ${heartRateCount} HR`
   497→      );
   498→
   499→      // Rate limit: 1s delay between chunks
   500→      if (chunks.indexOf(chunk) < chunks.length - 1) {
   501→        await sleep(1000);
   502→      }
   503→    } catch (e) {
   504→      const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error";
   505→      console.error(`Oura chunk ${chunk.start}~${chunk.end} failed: ${message}`);
   506→      if (message.includes("rate limit")) {
   507→        // Skip remaining chunks on rate limit
   508→        break;
   509→      }
   510→      // Other errors: skip this chunk and continue
   511→    }
   512→  }
   513→
   514→  // Update sync_state with last successful chunk end
   515→  await db
   516→    .prepare(
   517→      "UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = 'oura'"
   518→    )
   519→    .bind(lastSuccessfulEnd)
   520→    .run();
   521→
   522→  const total = totalSleep + totalActivity + totalReadiness + totalHeartRate;
   523→
   524→  return {
   525→    service: "oura",
   526→    success: true,
   527→    message: `Synced ${totalSleep} sleep, ${totalActivity} activity, ${totalReadiness} readiness, ${totalHeartRate} heart rate records to R2 Parquet`,
   528→    count: total,
   529→  };
   530→}
   531→
   532→// ============================================
   533→// Routes
   534→// ============================================
   535→
   536→// OAuth2 authorization
   537→app.get("/auth", async (c) => {
   538→  const state = crypto.randomUUID();
   539→
   540→  // Persist state for CSRF validation (expires in 10 minutes)
   541→  const stateExpiry = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
   542→  await c.env.DB.prepare(
   543→    `INSERT INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at)
   544→     VALUES ('oura_state', ?, '', ?)
   545→     ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET access_token = excluded.access_token, expires_at = excluded.expires_at`
   546→  )
   547→    .bind(state, stateExpiry)
   548→    .run();
   549→
   550→  const params = new URLSearchParams({
   551→    response_type: "code",
   552→    client_id: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,
   553→    redirect_uri: c.env.OURA_REDIRECT_URI,
   554→    scope: "daily heartrate",
   555→    state,
   556→  });
   557→  return c.redirect(`${OURA_AUTH_URL}?${params.toString()}`);
   558→});
   559→
   560→// OAuth2 callback
   561→app.get("/callback", async (c) => {
   562→  const code = c.req.query("code");
   563→  const state = c.req.query("state");
   564→  if (!code) {
   565→    return c.json({ error: "Missing authorization code" }, 400);
   566→  }
   567→
   568→  // Validate state for CSRF protection
   569→  const storedState = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   570→    "SELECT access_token, expires_at FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura_state'"
   571→  ).first<{ access_token: string; expires_at: string }>();
   572→
   573→  await c.env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM oauth_tokens WHERE id = 'oura_state'").run();
   574→
   575→  if (!storedState || storedState.access_token !== state) {
   576→    return c.json({ error: "Invalid state parameter" }, 400);
   577→  }
   578→  if (new Date(storedState.expires_at) < new Date()) {
   579→    return c.json({ error: "State expired, please retry authorization" }, 400);
   580→  }
   581→
   582→  const res = await fetch(OURA_TOKEN_URL, {
   583→    method: "POST",
   584→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
   585→    body: new URLSearchParams({
   586→      grant_type: "authorization_code",
   587→      code,
   588→      client_id: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_ID,
   589→      client_secret: c.env.OURA_CLIENT_SECRET,
   590→      redirect_uri: c.env.OURA_REDIRECT_URI,
   591→    }),
   592→  });
   593→
   594→  if (!res.ok) {
   595→    const text = await res.text();
   596→    return c.json({ error: "Token exchange failed", detail: text }, 500);
   597→  }
   598→
   599→  const token=[REDACTED] = await res.json();
   600→  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + token.expires_in * 1000).toISOString();
   601→
   602→  await c.env.DB.prepare(
   603→    `INSERT INTO oauth_tokens (id, access_token, refresh_token, token_type, expires_at, scope, updated_at)
   604→     VALUES ('oura', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
   605→     ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
   606→       access_token = excluded.access_token,
   607→       refresh_token = excluded.refresh_token,
   608→       token_type = excluded.token_type,
   609→       expires_at = excluded.expires_at,
   610→       scope = excluded.scope,
   611→       updated_at = excluded.updated_at`
   612→  )
   613→    .bind(token.access_token, token.refresh_token, token.token_type, expiresAt, token.scope ?? null)
   614→    .run();
   615→
   616→  return c.json({ success: true, message: "Oura connected successfully" });
   617→});
   618→
   619→// Manual sync
   620→app.post("/sync", async (c) => {
   621→  try {
   622→    const result = await runSync(c.env);
   623→    return c.json(result);
   624→  } catch (e) {
   625→    const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error";
   626→    const status = message.includes("not authenticated") ? 401 : 500;
   627→    return c.json({ service: "oura", success: false, message }, status);
   628→  }
   629→});
   630→
   631→// Stats — list R2 objects count
   632→app.get("/stats", async (c) => {
   633→  const tables = ["daily_sleep", "daily_activity", "daily_readiness", "heart_rate"];
   634→  const stats: Record<string, number> = {};
   635→
   636→  for (const table of tables) {
   637→    const list = await c.env.DATA_LAKE.list({ prefix: `oura/${table}/` });
   638→    stats[`${table}_files`] = list.objects.length;
   639→  }
   640→
   641→  const syncState = await c.env.DB.prepare(
   642→    "SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura'"
   643→  ).first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();
   644→
   645→  return c.json({ ...stats, last_sync: syncState?.last_sync_at ?? null });
   646→});
   647→
   648→export default app;
   649→
```

> AGENT

R2 binding `DATA_LAKE` is in wrangler.jsonc, so it's available in Workers Vitest pool tests via `env.DATA_LAKE`. Now I'll update the 4 failing sync tests to verify R2 writes instead of D1 queries.

The key changes:
1. Replace D1 `SELECT` assertions with `env.DATA_LAKE.get()` checks
2. The sync response now returns `count` as total across all data types
3. Need to set `last_sync_at` to a recent date so `splitIntoMonthlyChunks` only creates 1 chunk (otherwise mock interceptors won't match multiple chunk calls)

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
R2 isolated storage failure: `env.DATA_LAKE.get()` returns an `R2ObjectBody` with an unconsumed body stream. The Workers Vitest pool's isolated storage cleanup can't pop the R2 storage frame because of lingering SQLite WAL files. Using `head()` instead of `get()` avoids opening the body stream — it only returns metadata (size, key, etc.).
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Let me switch from `get()` to `head()` in the test assertions:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01YQTzkVToykgFd7tYQeWUHa

  "replace_all": true,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/ingestion/src/__tests__/oura.test.ts",
  "old_string": "env.DATA_LAKE.get(",
  "new_string": "env.DATA_LAKE.head("
}
```

> AGENT

Biomeの4つのwarningは `!` (non-null assertion) → `?` (optional chaining) の提案。テストコード内での `obj!.size` は前行で `not.toBeNull()` を検証済みなので安全です。`--unsafe` で修正すると意味的に異なるコード（`undefined > 0` = false が暗黙的にパスする可能性）になるため、そのままにします。

> AGENT

全テストパス。Task 4 完了です。変更したファイルのまとめ:

- **`ingestion/src/services/oura.ts`** — D1書き込み → R2 Parquet書き込みに全面リライト
- **`ingestion/src/__tests__/oura.test.ts`** — sync テストをR2検証に更新（`head()` でParquetファイル存在確認）
- **`ingestion/src/__tests__/env.d.ts`** — `DATA_LAKE`と`OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE`を型定義に追加
- **`ingestion/vitest.config.ts`** — `parquet.test.ts`をWorkers poolから除外

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- Workers Vitest poolでR2を使うテストでは、`get()` の代わりに `head()` を使うとisolated storageの問題を回避できます。`head()` はbodyストリームを開かないため、SQLite WALファイルの残存が起きません
- `splitIntoMonthlyChunks` が複数チャンクを生成するとfetchMockのインターセプターが足りなくなるため、テストでは `last_sync_at` を直近日にセットして1チャンクに制限しています
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

レビューが来ました。指摘を分析して対応します。

**Critical/High の指摘:**

1. **sync_state が全チャンク失敗時に巻き戻る** — `lastSuccessfulEnd` が `startDate` のままで更新される
2. **R2 list のページネーション** — 1000件超で正確なカウントが取れない

**Medium の指摘（対応する）:**

3. **heart-rate 30日ガード** — 月末チャンクで31日を超える場合がある
4. **チャンク失敗時に後続チャンク成功で失敗範囲がスキップされる**
5. **テストの日付がリアルタイム依存**
6. **`test:parquet` npm scriptがない**

対応不要と判断するもの:
- WASM import パス: Wrangler がこのパターンをサポートしている
- `tableFromArrays` の型推論: 実用上問題ない（Parquet読み取り側はスキーマ推論する）
- `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-03-11-d1-to-r2-iceberg.md` は別PRの成果物で今回無関係

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/ingestion/src/services/oura.ts",
  "old_string": "// Stats — list R2 objects count\napp.get(\"/stats\", async (c) => {\n  const tables = [\"daily_sleep\", \"daily_activity\", \"daily_readiness\", \"heart_rate\"];\n  const stats: Record<string, number> = {};\n\n  for (const table of tables) {\n    const list = await c.env.DATA_LAKE.list({ prefix: `oura/${table}/` });\n    stats[`${table}_files`] = list.objects.length;\n  }",
  "new_string": "// Stats — list R2 objects count (handles pagination)\napp.get(\"/stats\", async (c) => {\n  const tables = [\"daily_sleep\", \"daily_activity\", \"daily_readiness\", \"heart_rate\"];\n  const stats: Record<string, number> = {};\n\n  for (const table of tables) {\n    let count = 0;\n    let cursor: string | undefined;\n    do {\n      const list = await c.env.DATA_LAKE.list({ prefix: `oura/${table}/`, cursor });\n      count += list.objects.length;\n      cursor = list.truncated ? list.cursor : undefined;\n    } while (cursor);\n    stats[`${table}_files`] = count;\n  }"
}

     1→import type { Pipeline } from "cloudflare:pipelines";
     2→import type { Env } from "./types";
     3→
     4→const PAGE_SIZE = 10000;
     5→
     6→const ALLOWED_TABLES = ["oura_daily_sleep", "oura_daily_activity", "oura_daily_readiness"] as const;
     7→type AllowedTable = (typeof ALLOWED_TABLES)[number];
     8→
     9→function isAllowedTable(table: string): table is AllowedTable {
    10→  return (ALLOWED_TABLES as readonly string[]).includes(table);
    11→}
    12→
    13→async function getLastExportDate(db: D1Database): Promise<string | null> {
    14→  const row = await db
    15→    .prepare("SELECT last_sync_at FROM sync_state WHERE id = 'oura-export'")
    16→    .first<{ last_sync_at: string | null }>();
    17→  return row?.last_sync_at ?? null;
    18→}
    19→
    20→async function updateLastExportDate(db: D1Database, date: string): Promise<void> {
    21→  await db
    22→    .prepare("UPDATE sync_state SET last_sync_at = ? WHERE id = 'oura-export'")
    23→    .bind(date)
    24→    .run();
    25→}
    26→
    27→async function exportTable(
    28→  db: D1Database,
    29→  pipeline: Pipeline,
    30→  table: string,
    31→  lastExportDate: string | null
    32→): Promise<number> {
    33→  if (!isAllowedTable(table)) {
    34→    throw new Error(`Table not allowed: ${table}`);
    35→  }
    36→
    37→  const sql = lastExportDate
    38→    ? `SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC`
    39→    : `SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY day ASC`;
    40→  const result = lastExportDate
    41→    ? await db.prepare(sql).bind(lastExportDate).all()
    42→    : await db.prepare(sql).all();
    43→  const rows = result.results;
    44→
    45→  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;
    46→
    47→  const loadedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    48→  const enriched = rows.map((row) => ({ ...row, loaded_at: loadedAt }));
    49→  await pipeline.send(enriched);
    50→  return enriched.length;
    51→}
    52→
    53→async function exportHeartRate(
    54→  db: D1Database,
    55→  pipeline: Pipeline,
    56→  lastExportDate: string | null
    57→): Promise<number> {
    58→  let totalSent = 0;
    59→  let cursorDay = lastExportDate ?? "";
    60→  let cursorTimestamp = "";
    61→
    62→  while (true) {
    63→    let result: D1Result<Record<string, unknown>>;
    64→
    65→    if (cursorDay === "" && cursorTimestamp === "") {
    66→      result = await db
    67→        .prepare(`SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`)
    68→        .all();
    69→    } else if (cursorTimestamp === "") {
    70→      result = await db
    71→        .prepare(
    72→          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE day > ? ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`
    73→        )
    74→        .bind(cursorDay)
    75→        .all();
    76→    } else {
    77→      result = await db
    78→        .prepare(
    79→          `SELECT * FROM oura_heart_rate WHERE (day > ?) OR (day = ? AND timestamp > ?) ORDER BY day ASC, timestamp ASC LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE}`
    80→        )
    81→        .bind(cursorDay, cursorDay, cursorTimestamp)
    82→        .all();
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    const rows = result.results;
    86→    if (rows.length === 0) break;
    87→
    88→    const loadedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    89→    const enriched = rows.map((row) => ({ ...row, loaded_at: loadedAt }));
    90→    await pipeline.send(enriched);
    91→    totalSent += enriched.length;
    92→
    93→    const lastRow = rows[rows.length - 1] as { day: string; timestamp: string };
    94→    cursorDay = lastRow.day;
    95→    cursorTimestamp = lastRow.timestamp;
    96→
    97→    if (rows.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  return totalSent;
   101→}
   102→
   103→export async function runExport(env: Env): Promise<string> {
   104→  const lastExportDate = await getLastExportDate(env.DB);
   105→  const logs: string[] = [];
   106→
   107→  const sleepCount = await exportTable(
   108→    env.DB,
   109→    env.PIPELINE_SLEEP,
   110→    "oura_daily_sleep",
   111→    lastExportDate
   112→  );
   113→  logs.push(`daily_sleep: ${sleepCount} rows`);
   114→
   115→  const activityCount = await exportTable(
   116→    env.DB,
   117→    env.PIPELINE_ACTIVITY,
   118→    "oura_daily_activity",
   119→    lastExportDate
   120→  );
   121→  logs.push(`daily_activity: ${activityCount} rows`);
   122→
   123→  const readinessCount = await exportTable(
   124→    env.DB,
   125→    env.PIPELINE_READINESS,
   126→    "oura_daily_readiness",
   127→    lastExportDate
   128→  );
   129→  logs.push(`daily_readiness: ${readinessCount} rows`);
   130→
   131→  const heartRateCount = await exportHeartRate(env.DB, env.PIPELINE_HEART_RATE, lastExportDate);
   132→  logs.push(`heart_rate: ${heartRateCount} rows`);
   133→
   134→  const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
   135→  await updateLastExportDate(env.DB, today);
   136→
   137→  const summary = `Export complete: ${logs.join(", ")}`;
   138→  console.log(summary);
   139→  return summary;
   140→}
   141→
```

     1→import { runExport } from "./export";
     2→import type { Env } from "./types";
     3→
     4→export default {
     5→  async scheduled(_event: ScheduledEvent, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
     6→    ctx.waitUntil(runExport(env));
     7→  },
     8→
     9→  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    10→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    11→
    12→    if (url.pathname === "/health") {
    13→      return new Response("OK");
    14→    }
    15→
    16→    // 手動トリガー用 (デバッグ)
    17→    if (url.pathname === "/export" && request.method === "POST") {
    18→      const result = await runExport(env);
    19→      return new Response(result);
    20→    }
    21→
    22→    return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
    23→  },
    24→};

1. **D1クエリの冗長性** — `exportTable`のステートメント準備を統一

3. **`/export`エンドポイントのエラーハンドリング** — try/catch追加
4. **`last_sync_at`の更新タイミング** — 全チャンク失敗時のガード

}

     1→import { getSandbox } from "@cloudflare/sandbox";
     2→import type { DbtCommand, DbtCommandResult, DbtRunResult, Env, RunRequest } from "../types";
     3→
     4→const WORKSPACE = "/workspace";
     5→const ALLOWED_COMMANDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(["seed", "run", "test", "build"]);
     6→
     7→function generateRunId(): string {
     8→  const now = new Date();
     9→  const date = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, "");
    10→  const time = now.toISOString().slice(11, 19).replace(/:/g, "");
    11→  const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
    12→  return `${date}-${time}-${rand}`;
    13→}
    14→
    15→function cloneCommand(repo: string, ref: string, token: string): string {
    16→  const url = `https://x-access-token:${token}@github.com/${repo}.git`;
    17→  return `git clone --depth 1 --branch ${ref} ${url} ${WORKSPACE}/repo`;
    18→}
    19→
    20→function dbtCommand(dbtDir: string, command: DbtCommand): string {
    21→  if (!ALLOWED_COMMANDS.has(command)) {
    22→    throw new Error(`Invalid dbt command: ${command}`);
    23→  }
    24→  const fullPath = `${WORKSPACE}/repo/${dbtDir}`;
    25→  return [
    26→    `cd ${fullPath}`,
    27→    `set -a && source ${fullPath}/.env && set +a`,
    28→    `uv run dbt ${command} --profiles-dir . --target ci`,
    29→  ].join(" && ");
    30→}
    31→
    32→async function cloneRepo(
    33→  sandbox: ReturnType<typeof getSandbox>,
    34→  env: Env,
    35→  ref: string
    36→): Promise<{ success: boolean; commitSha: string; output: string }> {
    37→  const result = await sandbox.exec(cloneCommand(env.GITHUB_REPO, ref, env.GITHUB_TOKEN));
    38→  if (!result.success) {
    39→    return { success: false, commitSha: "", output: `${result.stdout}\n${result.stderr}` };
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const shaResult = await sandbox.exec(`cd ${WORKSPACE}/repo && git rev-parse HEAD`);
    43→  const commitSha = shaResult.stdout.trim();
    44→
    45→  return { success: true, commitSha, output: result.stdout };
    46→}
    47→
    48→async function setupEnv(
    49→  sandbox: ReturnType<typeof getSandbox>,
    50→  env: Env,
    51→  dbtDir: string
    52→): Promise<void> {
    53→  const envContent = [
    54→    `R2_ENDPOINT=${env.R2_ENDPOINT}`,
    55→    `R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID=${env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID}`,
    56→    `R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY=${env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY}`,
    57→  ].join("\n");
    58→
    59→  await sandbox.writeFile(`${WORKSPACE}/repo/${dbtDir}/.env`, envContent);
    60→}
    61→
    62→async function installDeps(
    63→  sandbox: ReturnType<typeof getSandbox>,
    64→  dbtDir: string
    65→): Promise<{ success: boolean; output: string; durationMs: number }> {
    66→  const fullPath = `${WORKSPACE}/repo/${dbtDir}`;
    67→  const start = Date.now();
    68→  const result = await sandbox.exec(
    69→    `cd ${fullPath} && uv sync --frozen --no-dev && uv run dbt deps --profiles-dir . --target ci`
    70→  );
    71→  return {
    72→    success: result.success,
    73→    output: result.stdout + (result.stderr ? `\n--- stderr ---\n${result.stderr}` : ""),
    74→    durationMs: Date.now() - start,
    75→  };
    76→}
    77→
    78→export async function runDbt(
    79→  env: Env,
    80→  request: RunRequest = {}
    81→): Promise<{ result: DbtRunResult; artifacts: Record<string, string> }> {
    82→  const ref = request.ref ?? "main";
    83→  const commands = request.commands ?? ["seed", "run", "test"];
    84→  const dbtDir = request.dbtDir ?? "transform/core";
    85→
    86→  const runId = generateRunId();
    87→  const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    88→  const sandbox = getSandbox(env.SANDBOX, `dbt-run-${runId}`);
    89→
    90→  // 1. Clone repo
    91→  const clone = await cloneRepo(sandbox, env, ref);
    92→  if (!clone.success) {
    93→    await sandbox.destroy();
    94→    return {
    95→      result: {
    96→        runId,
    97→        ref,
    98→        commitSha: "",
    99→        startedAt,
   100→        completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   101→        success: false,
   102→        commands: [],
   103→        error: `git clone failed: ${clone.output}`,
   104→      },
   105→      artifacts: {},
   106→    };
   107→  }
   108→
   109→  // 2. Write .env and install dependencies
   110→  await setupEnv(sandbox, env, dbtDir);
   111→  const deps = await installDeps(sandbox, dbtDir);
   112→  if (!deps.success) {
   113→    await sandbox.destroy();
   114→    return {
   115→      result: {
   116→        runId,
   117→        ref,
   118→        commitSha: clone.commitSha,
   119→        startedAt,
   120→        completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   121→        success: false,
   122→        commands: [],
   123→        error: `dependency install failed: ${deps.output}`,
   124→      },
   125→      artifacts: {},
   126→    };
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  // 3. Run dbt commands
   130→  const commandResults: DbtCommandResult[] = [];
   131→  let allSuccess = true;
   132→
   133→  for (const cmd of commands) {
   134→    const fullCmd = dbtCommand(dbtDir, cmd);
   135→    const start = Date.now();
   136→    const sandboxResult = await sandbox.exec(fullCmd);
   137→
   138→    const cmdResult: DbtCommandResult = {
   139→      command: cmd,
   140→      success: sandboxResult.success,
   141→      output:
   142→        sandboxResult.stdout +
   143→        (sandboxResult.stderr ? `\n--- stderr ---\n${sandboxResult.stderr}` : ""),
   144→      durationMs: Date.now() - start,
   145→    };
   146→
   147→    commandResults.push(cmdResult);
   148→
   149→    if (!sandboxResult.success) {
   150→      allSuccess = false;
   151→      break;
   152→    }
   153→  }
   154→
   155→  // 4. Collect artifacts
   156→  const artifacts: Record<string, string> = {};
   157→  const dbtPath = `${WORKSPACE}/repo/${dbtDir}`;
   158→  const artifactPaths = [
   159→    { key: "manifest.json", path: `${dbtPath}/target/manifest.json` },
   160→    { key: "run_results.json", path: `${dbtPath}/target/run_results.json` },
   161→    { key: "dbt.log", path: `${dbtPath}/logs/dbt.log` },
   162→  ];
   163→
   164→  for (const { key, path } of artifactPaths) {
   165→    try {
   166→      const file = await sandbox.readFile(path);
   167→      artifacts[key] = file.content;
   168→    } catch {
   169→      // Artifact may not exist if dbt didn't reach that stage
   170→    }
   171→  }
   172→
   173→  await sandbox.destroy();
   174→
   175→  const result: DbtRunResult = {
   176→    runId,
   177→    ref,
   178→    commitSha: clone.commitSha,
   179→    startedAt,
   180→    completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   181→    success: allSuccess,
   182→    commands: commandResults,
   183→  };
   184→
   185→  return { result, artifacts };
   186→}
   187→
```

> AGENT

This warning is a false positive — `sandbox.exec()` is the Cloudflare Sandbox API, not Node.js `child_process.exec()`. The input validation we're adding (`validateRef`, `validateDbtDir`) is exactly the mitigation for this concern.

3つのPRすべてマージ完了しました。

- **PR #51** (D1 to R2 Iceberg) — マージ済み
- **PR #52** (Oura R2 Parquet) — マージ済み
- **PR #49** (Sandbox dbt Worker) — マージ済み

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **Shell injection防御**: Cloudflare Sandboxの`exec()`はシェルコマンドをそのまま実行するため、`validateRef()`でgit refに安全な文字のみ許可するregexバリデーションが重要。`execFile`パターンが使えない環境では入力バリデーションが唯一の防御線になります。
- **squash merge戦略**: 各PRを`--squash`でマージすることで、レビュー修正やconflict解決の中間コミットがmainに混入せず、クリーンな1コミット=1機能の履歴を維持できます。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`
